match between the activation distribution and the discrete levels of a low-bit uniform quantizer.","importance":"This is an inference-efficiency paper rather than a pretraining paper, but it is useful for deployment and post-training compression. Its main value is the distribution-design view of activation transformation. Instead of only suppressing outliers or minimizing reconstruction MSE, it asks what distribution a 4-bit uniform quantizer can preserve well. This explains why some transformations can make activations look smoother while still causing large degradation: the quantization range may remain too wide, or most values may collapse into only a few levels near the mean.","technical_approach":"InfoQuant is a train-free PTQ method that learns orthogonal transformations to reshape activation distributions. The paper analyzes round-to-nearest uniform quantization from an information-theoretic angle and identifies two desirable properties: a smaller numerical range and higher normalized dispersion within that range. The first property prevents rare extremes from inflating the quantization step size; the second prevents dense central values from collapsing into too few quantization bins. The main component is Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation, which optimizes an activation-side objective to reduce peaks while increasing dispersion. The method also uses adaptive outlier-token selection for more stable calibration and learns activation clipping parameters after distribution shaping. It focuses on activation optimization and remains compatible with standard weight-quantization pipelines.","experiments":"The reported setting is W4A4KV4, where weights, activations, and KV cache are all quantized to 4 bits. Experiments include LLaMA-2 7B, 13B, and 70B, as well as LLaMA-3 8B and 70B. The models retain about 97% of their original performance on average. The paper also reports that a 70B model can be quantized with only 24GB of GPU memory. The key empirical point is that explicitly shaping the activation distribution toward smaller range and better within-range dispersion is more aligned with low-bit activation quantization than objectives based only on outlier suppression or MSE reconstruction.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 15–25 minutes. QAT 里一个老问题：前向量化是硬离散的，反向传播却长期靠 STE 把量化器当恒等映射处理。bit 数降到 2bit 甚至更低时，这个近似会明显失真，因为大量权重靠近 bin 边界或进入 clipping/saturation 区域，真实量化器对参数扰动几乎不敏感，但 STE 仍然把梯度原样传回去，导致训练震荡、收敛差、低精度模型行为和训练信号错位。作者要解决的是，能不能在不改动硬量化前向的前提下，用一个便宜、可学习的 Jacobian 代理替代 STE 的假梯度。","importance":"对基座模型研究的意义主要在训练与部署接口。QAT 虽然更常被归到压缩/部署，但当模型规模变大、推理成本成为一等约束时，超低比特训练已经不只是后处理技巧，而是模型生命周期的一部分。JacQuant 的启发点在于，它把 QAT 重新表述成“缺失 Jacobian”的问题，而不是继续围绕 STE 做经验修补。这个视角对低精度预训练、量化 mid-train、甚至其他含离散算子的训练目标都有参考价值。贡献量级上，它更像是一个干净的算法替换件：不改前向量化器，不引入很重的辅助网络，用组级敏感度近似就能改善 ≤2bit 区间的稳定性和精度。","technical_approach":"方法核心很直接：保留部署时真正使用的硬量化器 QΔ(·) 做前向，但把反向里的 identity surrogate 换成一个学习得到的敏感度映射 B(W)。若 q=QΔ(W)，损失对量化权重的梯度是 v=∇qℓ，则 JacQuant 用 ∇Wℓ≈B(W)v，而不是 STE 的 ∇Wℓ≈Iv。B(W) 被约束成对角或块对角结构；在默认设置里是 group-wise block diagonal，每个量化组只学一个标量增益 bg，因此额外状态极小，比如 group size 128 时，每 128 个权重只多一个标量。这个设计的关键不是提高表达力，而是抓住量化器局部灵敏度最重要的一维信息：在 bin 内部，扰动更可能穿过量化映射，增益接近 1；在饱和/裁剪区域，真实响应应接近 0，梯度也应被抑制。作者给了两种低开销估计器。JacQuant-Probe 用随机高斯扰动 δ 测量 Q(W+δ)-Q(W) 的变化，再做一阶 slope 拟合，估计每个组的平均敏感度；JacQuant-Dither 用 subtractive dithering，把硬量化嵌入一个平滑化的随机模型中，再估计均值 Jacobian。B(W) 不需要每步更新，而是在 refresh step 上间歇更新，并在多步 QAT 中复用，因此吞吐损失较小。理论部分也围绕这个平滑化视角展开：在 code-preserving 训练阶段，如果学到的 B(W) 能跟踪 mean sensitivity，则 JacQuant 的更新可对应到一个定义良好的目标梯度；在非凸情形下给出标准收敛保证，在满足 PŁ 条件时给出线性收敛率。和 Rotation Trick 这类固定几何修正不同，JacQuant 的 backward gain 是数据驱动、位置相关、随训练状态变化的。","experiments":"从引言能确认的实验范围包括：LLaMA3-1B、LLaMA3-3B、Qwen3-1.7B，以及一个 LLaMA3-8B 的 W2A16 训练轨迹；对比对象包括标准 STE 和在 matched ParetoQ 设置下的 Rotation Trick；还报告了 LLaMA3-1B 与 8B 上的运行时开销。作者把经验结论明确限定在 ultra-low-bit LLM QAT，尤其是 ≤2bit 权重量化区间。摘要和正文前段给出的主结论是，JacQuant 在这些设置下同时改善训练稳定性和最终精度，并且额外开销很小。由于给出的全文片段没有展开完整结果表，无法可靠复述具体 benchmark 数字、困惑度或下游提升幅度；但可以确定作者强调了两点：一是收益主要出现在 STE 最失真的区域，也就是低 bit、边界/饱和现象更严重时；二是 JacQuant 作为 drop-in backward rule，可在现有 QAT pipeline 中替换 STE，而不需要重写前向量化或引入重型二阶近似。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，把作者为何把问题定义成 missing Jacobian 而不是“改进 STE”理解清楚。然后直接看 Method 里 B(W) 的参数化方式、Probe 与 Dither 两种估计器、refresh 频率设计，这三处决定了方法是否真能落地。若时间有限，理论部分只需抓住一句：他们不是在证明硬量化器可导，而是在平滑/抖动模型下给一个有定义的目标梯度，并说明当 surrogate 跟踪均值敏感度时优化是稳定的。实验部分重点看 ≤2bit 设置、与 Rotation Trick 的对比、以及 runtime overhead。预计 20–30 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读；如果你关心低比特继续训练或量化预训练，值得再细看 surrogate 更新频率和 group size 的消融。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a long-standing weakness of ultra-low-bit quantization-aware training. In standard QAT, the forward pass uses a hard quantizer, but the backward pass usually relies on the straight-through estimator, which simply treats the quantizer as identity. That approximation becomes especially poor at 2-bit and below, where many weights sit near bin boundaries or in clipping/saturation regions. The true quantizer is then locally insensitive, while STE still propagates full gradients. The result is unstable optimization and a mismatch between the low-precision model behavior and the training signal. The paper asks whether that missing Jacobian can be replaced by a cheap learned surrogate without changing the deployed hard quantizer.","importance":"For foundation-model work, the relevance is at the training–deployment boundary. QAT is often framed as a compression technique, but once inference cost becomes a first-class constraint, ultra-low-bit adaptation is part of the model lifecycle rather than a purely downstream concern. The useful idea here is conceptual: QAT is cast as a missing-Jacobian problem, not as another heuristic patch on top of STE. That framing may transfer beyond quantization, to other training settings with discrete operators or hard projections. In practical terms, the contribution is a clean algorithmic replacement for the backward rule: keep the same forward quantizer, add a lightweight group-wise sensitivity model, and improve stability in the ≤2-bit regime.","technical_approach":"$a","experiments":"From the provided full-text content, the empirical scope includes LLaMA3-1B, LLaMA3-3B, Qwen3-1.7B, and a LLaMA3-8B W2A16 trajectory. Baselines include standard STE and Rotation Trick under matched ParetoQ settings, and runtime overhead is reported on both LLaMA3-1B and LLaMA3-8B. The authors explicitly scope their claims to ultra-low-bit LLM QAT, especially the ≤2-bit weight regime. The stated empirical outcome is that JacQuant improves both training stability and final accuracy while adding little overhead. The available text does not include the full result tables, so it would be unsafe to invent exact benchmark numbers or perplexity gains. What is clear is where the gains come from: the method helps most where STE is most misaligned, namely low-bit settings with frequent boundary crossings and saturation. It is also presented as a drop-in replacement for the backward rule, rather than a redesign of the forward quantizer or a heavy second-order method.","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and make sure the problem framing is clear: this is a missing-Jacobian formulation, not just another STE tweak. Then go straight to the method section and focus on three things: how B(W) is parameterized, how the Probe and Dither estimators work, and how often the surrogate is refreshed. Those choices determine whether the method is actually practical. If time is limited, the theory section only needs one takeaway: the paper does not claim hard quantization is differentiable; it defines a principled target gradient through a smoothed or dithered model and shows stable optimization when the surrogate tracks mean sensitivity. In experiments, prioritize the ≤2-bit results, the comparison against Rotation Trick, and the runtime overhead. A useful skim should take about 20–30 minutes. The recipe is one math-invariant transformation: WHT-rotate each linear layer's weight matrix and rescale its columns by per-coordinate Walsh-basis activation energy before handing off to a reconstruction-error quantizer (Intel auto-round). 负责把表示转到谱域，适配性不来自学习旋转，而来自对角缩放；这点是它和 ButterflyQuant、SpinQuant 一类学习旋转方法的主要区别。论文还处理了若干架构兼容问题：Qwen3 中用 spectral-PCA 替代 post-projection norm 的矩阵 Γ；非 norm 架构中使用与 RoPE 结构相容的 pair-PCA，即每个 RoPE pair 内的 SO(2) reconstruction-error quantizers allocate rounding budget without enough awareness of which channels carry more signal. This is especially damaging in W2A16, where the quantization noise budget is very tight.","importance":"Its value is mainly on the deployment and compression side, with some indirect relevance to pretraining through model robustness under low-bit constraints. The contribution is a math-equivalent pre-quantization transform that injects a channel-importance signal into an existing quantizer without learning a rotation or changing the model. The claims are appropriately limited: the Boolean influence theory is used as motivation rather than a formal guarantee for real-valued LLM layers, and the empirical comparison is against vanilla auto-round rather than matched strong baselines such as AWQ, GPTQ, SpinQuant, QuaRot, or QuIP#.","technical_approach":"The method rotates each linear layer into a Walsh-Hadamard spectral basis, rescales columns according to per-coordinate spectral activation energy, and then applies Intel auto-round. The scaling has the form s_l=(1+d rho_l)^alpha, where rho_l measures spectral activation energy. The fixed WHT provides the spectral coordinate system; adaptivity comes from the diagonal rescaling rather than from a learned orthogonal transform. This is the main distinction from learned-rotation approaches such as SpinQuant or ButterflyQuant. The paper also adds engineering extensions for harder architectures: spectral-PCA for Qwen3 post-projection norm handling, RoPE-compatible pair-PCA rotations for non-norm architectures, and MoE-aware input-side absorption for fused 3D expert layouts. Full-precision invariance is numerically checked with relative error below 5e-7.","experiments":"The main evaluation uses SmolLM-135M, SmolLM-360M, Qwen2.5-0.5B, and Qwen2.5-1.5B, with an auxiliary TinyLlama-1.1B result. In W2A16, the method improves wikitext-2 perplexity by roughly 15% to 58% over vanilla auto-round, with larger gains on models where the W2 baseline degrades more severely. A W4 ablation shows that the gains mostly fall within the reported ±0.5 PPL noise floor, which supports the interpretation that the transform matters most when per-channel rounding budget is scarce. An architecture-fuzzing experiment based on poolside Laguna-XS.2, a 33B MoE model that was too expensive to run fully, exposed and fixed a generalization bug in input-side attention absorption around a g_proj input-side gate pattern. The results are single-seed, and the paper does not claim standard statistical significance.","reading_guide":"A skim is enough, about 20 to 30 minutes. 低比特化如果损伤长程检索和复制能力，会影响数据生成、评测和 post-train 迭代。ThriftAttention 给出一个实用判断：不必把所有 attention 都保留高精度，也不应盲目全部降到 FP4；更合理的是把有限高精度预算分配给对 softmax 输出最敏感的交互块。","technical_approach":"方法是训练无关的选择性混合精度 attention。论文先从 softmax 的一阶扰动分析出发，说明单个 key 的误差贡献近似由三项决定：score 量化误差、attention weight，以及 value 与最终输出的偏差。其中 attention weight 会放大高分 token 的量化误差，因此高注意力块更值得保留 FP16。具体实现中，Q、K、V 使用 Blackwell 支持的 NVFP4 microscaling 格式量化，元素为 E2M1 FP4，scale 为 E4M3 FP8。然后将 Q 和 K/V 分块，对每个 query block 和 key block 计算 token mean 的点积作为重要性分数：S_ij = mean(Q_i) · mean(K_j)。每个 query block 选择 top-k key block 用 FP16 计算，其余 block 用 FP4 计算。两路结果通过 online softmax 合并成一个 attention 输出。与稀疏 attention 不同，它没有丢弃低重要性 block，只是降低其精度，因此避免了 aggressive sparsity 下不可恢复的信息丢失。","experiments":"论文在 LongBench-v1、HELMET、RULER 和 PG-19 上评测，覆盖 Llama、Qwen 和 Ministral 系列模型。关键结果是，在仅 5% FP16 block 预算下，ThriftAttention 平均恢复 FP4 到 FP16 性能差距的 89.1%；预算提升到 10% 和 25% 时，恢复比例分别为 91.8% 和 92.4%。端到端生成中，长上下文场景的推理延迟最高降低约 2 倍。序列长度分析显示，上下文越长，uniform FP4 的退化越明显，选择性混合精度的收益也越大。论文还通过可视化说明 FP16 与 FP4 attention 概率差异集中在少数 query-key block，常见于近对角区域和非初始 attention sink。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–35 分钟。Method 的一阶误差分析是核心，重点看为什么 attention weight 会让误差集中化。实现部分要看 NVFP4 格式、block 划分、top-k block 选择和 online softmax 合并。实验部分建议优先看不同 FP16 block budget 的质量恢复曲线，以及长上下文长度变化下的退化趋势。若关注 serving 与预训练闭环，值得深挖它是否会影响长程复制、needle retrieval、代码仓库理解这类对少数关键 token 极敏感的任务。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles the quality loss of FP4 attention in long-context inference. Its central observation is that quantization error is not uniformly harmful: the output-relevant error is concentrated in a small number of high-attention query-key blocks.","importance":"This is primarily an inference-efficiency paper, but it matters for pretraining workflows that depend on large-scale long-context evaluation, synthetic data generation, and post-training iteration. It argues against both uniform FP4 attention and overly aggressive sparsity. A better trade-off is to spend a small FP16 budget on the interactions most sensitive to softmax perturbations while keeping the rest in FP4.","technical_approach":"$b","experiments":"The evaluation covers LongBench-v1, HELMET, RULER, and PG-19 across Llama, Qwen, and Ministral model families. rubric，因为它可解释、可审计、便于拆维度。这篇工作的价值在于，它给出了一组较干净的对照证据：在高专业、难枚举标准的任务里，pairwise judgment 往往比 rubric 更能保留专家的整体判断，而且单位时间信息密度更高。这个结论会影响两类事情。第一，如何构造高质量 reward signal，尤其是在 reasoning、写作、专业知识工作这类“好坏能感觉到，但很难事先写全标准”的任务上。第二，如何理解自动评测的上限：如果人类专家本身更稳定地给出相对偏好，而不是绝对分数，那么让 LLM autograder 学 preference 可能比学 rubric 更顺。贡献量级偏方法论和评测基础设施，不是训练范式级别的改变，但对高质量监督数据建设有现实参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个关键设计。第一，任务来源尽量贴近真实专业工作。作者从 Harvey 的 BigLaw Bench 中选取 30 个法律任务，覆盖 litigation 和 transactional 场景，保留 prompt、相关文档和任务 rubric。与常见考试式法律 benchmark 不同，这些任务更像真实律师工作产物。第二，输出质量不是直接拿现成模型自然分布，而是人为构造三档质量层级的答案，用来形成一个可检验的“已知顺序”。从摘要和正文可见，作者使用 LLM 生成不同质量水平的候选输出，再让专家分别做 rubric 评分和两两偏好判断。这样做的目的不是模拟真实线上分布，而是让评估方法是否能恢复质量排序变成一个可测问题。第三，配对标注设计。最关键的是同一任务、同一输出集合，由同一类专家同时提供 rubric 分数和 pairwise preference。rubric 侧是基于任务特定的原子化标准逐项给分，包含正向加分项和负向扣分项；preference 侧则让律师在两个候选答案之间选更优者。作者还进一步比较了人类标注和 LLM autograder 在这两种监督形式下的表现。论文的核心判断不是“rubric 没用”，而是指出在 tacit knowledge 占比高、质量维度难预先枚举时，rubric 会把专家判断压缩成一组不完全的显式标准，信息损失更大；而 pairwise judgment 更接近专家的整体比较过程。代价是 preference 更难直接审计和拆解成可复用规则。","experiments":"实验主结论很清楚：comparative judgment 在恢复构造的质量顺序上持续优于 rubric，而且标注时间不到后者的一半。数据规模方面，论文释放了 30 个真实法律任务，包含 1,539 条 rubric scores 和 1,530 条 pairwise preference judgments，标注来自有实务经验的执业律师，作者按美国大所计费口径估算，相当于约 242 小时、约 24.2 万美元的律师工时。结果层面，正文强调这一优势同时出现在人工评估和 LLM 自动评分上：也就是说，不只是人类更容易做 preference，模型在学习或模仿这种监督信号时也更稳定。论文还讨论了时间成本，指出 rubric 的前期设计和逐项核对都更耗时，而 preference 只要求专家做相对判断，因此单位时间可获得更多可靠监督。更值得注意的是，这个结论是在作者刻意构造的三档质量输出上得到的，因此它测的是“能否识别质量差异”，不是开放世界中的绝对分数校准。对做 reward modeling 的人来说，这一点很重要：论文支持 preference 在排序和相对优劣识别上的优势，但并不自动推出它在所有需要可解释分解、错误归因、合规审计的场景里都优于 rubric。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Dataset Construction，抓住论文真正比较的对象：不是法律能力，而是两种监督信号。然后重点看实验部分里关于 constructed quality ordering 的定义，以及人类与 LLM autograder 两条结果线是否一致。若时间有限，优先看 Figure 1 的数据构造流程、主结果表，以及关于 annotation time 的统计。阅读时要特别留意两个边界。第一，任务来自法律高专业场景，结论更适用于“标准难写全”的任务，不一定直接外推到事实核查、格式检查这类 rubric 很自然的任务。第二，作者比较的是 paired annotations，这个设计很干净，但样本量仍然不大，且输出质量层级带有人为构造痕迹。预计 20–30 分钟可完成泛读；如果关心 reward modeling 或 judge model，建议再细看作者如何定义“recover quality ordering”，以及 LLM autograder 是如何从 rubric / preference 信号中做预测的。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a methodological question rather than a legal one: in high-expertise tasks where quality is multidimensional and hard to fully specify upfront, should we rely on rubric-based scoring or pairwise preference judgments as the supervision signal? Law is used as the testbed because legal work products are strongly context-dependent, often resist objective decomposition, and make “quality” unusually hard to operationalize. The key design is a paired comparison: the same legal tasks, the same model outputs, and the same type of expert annotators are used to collect both rubric scores and comparative judgments, so the study can isolate differences between the two evaluation paradigms instead of conflating them with task or annotator variation.","importance":"$c","technical_approach":"$d","experiments":"$e","reading_guide":"$f","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"对于做评测或对齐的研究员非常重要：在高专业领域（法律），pairwise偏好比rubric评分的效度高得多（0.908 vs 0.150），这对设计评测协议和RL对齐数据标注策略有直接参考意义。","en":"Highly relevant for researchers doing evaluation or alignment: in high-expertise domains (law), pairwise preferences are vastly more valid than rubric scoring (0.908 vs 0.150), with direct implications for evaluation protocol design and RL alignment data annotation strategies."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Stanford University"],"authors":["Russell Yang","Ruishi Chen","Pierce Kelaita","Riya Ranjan","Sibo Ma","Charles Dickens","Matthew Guillod","Megan Ma","Julian Nyarko"],"notable_authors":["Julian Nyarko"],"tags":["evaluation","benchmark","preference-evaluation","rubric-scoring","legal-domain"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25240","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.25240","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.25133","title":"Trust but Verify: Prover-Verifier Deliberation for Selective LLM Prediction","abstract":"Reliably knowing when a language model is correct is almost as important as being correct. We introduce prover-verifier deliberation (PVD), an inference-time protocol grounded in interactive proof theory, as a mechanism for selective prediction: the protocol produces both an answer and a structured confidence verdict, allowing a system to report high-confidence answers while abstaining on uncertain cases. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-24T15:23:27+00:00","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25133.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文关注 LLM 的选择性预测：模型不仅要回答问题，还要知道哪些答案可以高置信输出、哪些应该拒答。核心问题不是提高平均准确率，而是把推理过程转化为可用的置信信号。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献有限，但对 post-train、推理时计算和 verifier 设计有参考意义。它把 interactive proof 的 prover-verifier 结构移植到冻结 LLM 的推理流程中，用结构化挑战替代简单自一致性投票。价值在于提醒：verifier 不一定只服务于最终打分，也可以通过多轮质询产生覆盖率-精度曲线，用于判断模型输出是否可用。这对后续训练 verifier、构造过程监督数据、设计 abstention-aware evaluation 都有启发。","technical_approach":"PVD 使用两个冻结模型，一个作为 prover，一个作为 verifier。prover 先给出候选答案，并把理由拆成 3–5 个可检查的 atomic sub-claims。verifier 不直接给置信分，而是在每轮给出 Accept、Challenge 或 Reject。Challenge 时，verifier 选择最可疑或最缺证据的子命题发问；prover 需要补充论证，也允许修改答案。若 verifier 接受且答案没有被修改，则记为 84–98%，覆盖率为 43–77%；相对非 ANC 样本，精度高出 6.6–34.8 个百分点。最强信号来自同系列但能力不完全对称的组合：GPT-5.4 prover 配 GPT-5.4-mini verifier，ANC 精度 97.6%，覆盖率 43%，比非 ANC 样本高 34.8 个百分点。HLE 上的结果更不稳定，弱 prover-verifier 组合可能出现 训练，可以深挖 prover 子命题拆解和 verifier targeted challenge 的 transcript，这些可能转化为过程监督或 verifier preference 数据。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies selective prediction for LLMs: a model should not only answer a question, but also identify when its answer is reliable enough to report. The target is calibrated coverage rather than average accuracy alone.","importance":"The work is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to post-training interfaces, verifier design, and inference-time compute. Its main contribution is to turn a prover-verifier interaction into a confidence signal. Instead of using agreement among samples as a weak proxy for correctness, it uses targeted challenges over decomposed claims. This is useful for thinking about abstention-aware evaluation, process supervision data, and future verifier training.","technical_approach":"PVD uses two frozen LLMs. The prover proposes an answer and decomposes its justification into 3–5 atomic, checkable sub-claims. The verifier then returns one of three decisions at each round: Accept, Challenge, or Reject. A Challenge targets the most suspicious or least supported sub-claim, and the prover must defend it or revise the answer. The key operating point is Accept + No Change, or ANC: cases where the verifier accepts the proof and the prover never changes the answer. ANC is treated as a high-confidence subset for selective prediction. Compared with self-consistency, the signal comes from adversarial checking rather than vote agreement. Compared with debate, the protocol is more constrained: claims are decomposed, challenges are targeted, and the verifier must issue an explicit verdict.","experiments":"The paper evaluates PVD on GPQA Diamond and Humanity’s Last Exam, with comparisons to self-consistency with k=8, multi-agent debate, Universal Self-Consistency, and Reflexion. On GPQA Diamond, ANC examples reach 84–98% high-confidence precision at 43–77% coverage, with a 6.6–34.8 percentage-point gap over the non-ANC complement. The strongest signal comes from a within-family asymmetric pairing: GPT-5.4 as prover and GPT-5.4-mini as verifier, giving 97.6% ANC precision at 43% coverage, 34.8 points above the complement. On HLE, weak prover-verifier pairings can collapse or even invert the Accept/Reject accuracy gap. This failure mode is important: the protocol does not inherit formal soundness from interactive proof theory; it only works as an empirical calibration mechanism when the verifier is competent enough.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough. Start with the introduction for the selective prediction framing and the comparison with self-consistency and debate. Then read the PVD protocol carefully, especially the ANC definition, the Challenge/Reject criteria, fatigue termination, and retry mechanism. In the experiments, focus on the GPQA coverage-precision results and the HLE failure cases. V","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T02:41:50+00:00","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25364.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“MLLM 的目标就是构造一套对这种“语言替代”不友好的评测，让题目必须依赖视觉结构、空间关系、局部差异、图形规则等直接视觉证据，才能较稳定地答对。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在评测诊断，而不在新训练方法。它提供了一个更干净的信号，帮助区分“视觉输入被真正用到了多少”与“模型只是把图像翻译成文本后继续做语言推理”这两件事。对做 native multimodal pretrain、视觉 tokenizer、跨模态对齐 loss、视觉 reasoning 数据构造的人，这类 benchmark 有实际价值，因为它能暴露训练目标是否真的提升了视觉 grounding，而不是只提升了语言外壳下的答题能力。它的贡献量级属于中等偏上：不是范式改变，但在当前 MLLM 评测里补上了一个长期缺口。","technical_approach":"方法上核心是 benchmark 设计与验证。作者先定义 10 个视觉推理大类、36 个细粒度子类，覆盖 perceptual、structural、conceptual 三层能力。题目来源于在线视觉推理资源和公务员考试题库，随后由人工改写成统一 VQA 格式，答案类型分成选择、填空、开放问答和 2D 框选四类。每个样本至少经过多名标注者独立判断与复核，保证题面、答案和格式一致性。真正有意思的部分不只是数据收集，而是作者用“图像替换为 caption”的对照实验去验证 benchmark 的 vision-centric 属性：如果一个题目在 caption-only 条件下仍能做对，那它就不够依赖视觉证据；VisReason 在这个测试下出现了 48.12% 的显著性能下降，说明它比现有 明显更依赖原始视觉输入。第二，人类与模型差距很大，且不同模型之间被明显拉开，说明它既不是过难到全体接近随机，也不是过易到无法区分能力。第三，test-time reasoning budget 增加能带来持续但缓慢且趋于饱和的提升；论文没有把它包装成“多想就能解决”，而是明确指出额外计算无法替代视觉 grounding。第四，对非-thinking 模型，显式 CoT 的平均收益很小，例如 GPT-4o 只有约 +1.1%，而且在 perception-heavy 类别上并不稳定。另一个稳定现象是模型规模越大，VisReason 表现越好，但即便最大模型也明显落后于人类，说明单纯 scaling 还不够。作为 skim 论文，最值得记住的不是某个绝对分数，而是这几个实验共同支持的判断：现有 MLLM caption substitution，三是 inference-time scaling 与 CoT 的收益曲线。若时间有限，30 分钟左右足够抓住主线。若要深挖，重点想两个问题：第一，VisReason 是否真的排除了语言先验，只是“更难 caption”还是“必须看图”；第二，这类 benchmark 对预训练 recipe 的可优化性有多强，也就是它能否反向指导数据和目标函数设计，而不只是做诊断。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a sharper question than standard multimodal evaluation: when MLLMs succeed on visual reasoning tasks, are they actually reasoning from visual evidence, or are they mostly converting images into language and then solving the problem in text space? The authors show that on benchmarks such as MMMU, MMMU-Pro, and MathVista, replacing the image with a model-generated caption causes less than a 6.35% drop, which suggests that much of the reasoning can survive language mediation. VisReason is built to make that shortcut fail: the tasks require direct use of visual structure, spatial layout, local differences, and implicit visual rules.","importance":"Its value is diagnostic rather than algorithmic. We term this phenomenon Structural Cognitive Overload (SCO), a byproduct of the contention between deep reasoning and safety alignment. However, prior work has predominantly targeted typographic and pixel-level perturbations, leaving the study of SCO largely unexplored. To this end, we propose StructBreak, an automated end-to-end framework designed to quan","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T07:41:51+00:00","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25534.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 MLLM 在复杂结构化输入下的安全失效：当模型需要解析视觉知识图、流程图式结构和多跳关系时，结构推理负载可能压过安全对齐信号，导致原本应拒答的有害请求被执行。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在训练方法，而在揭示多模态模型的一个评测与安全盲区。它提示模型能力越强，尤其是图结构解析和结构推理越强，未必天然更安全。安全对齐如果主要依赖文本拒答模式、OCR 防御或表层扰动鲁棒性，可能覆盖不到高结构负载场景。对 native multimodal pretraining 和图文统一建模来说，这是一个需要纳入训练数据、对齐数据和评测协议的风险类型。","technical_approach":"StructBreak 采用黑盒 image-text 攻击设定，不需要梯度、logits 或模型内部状态。核心载体是 Visual Knowledge Graphs。恶意意图不直接放在文本指令中，而是编码进图结构、节点关系和推理路径里；文本提示则伪装成较正常的任务触发器，例如学术分析、系统调试或结构解释。框架包含生成、过滤、评估流程：先按风险类别选择模板，将有害请求改写为结构化场景；再生成具有足够认知密度的 VKG；随后通过反馈调整图结构复杂度，使输入接近模型安全失效的阈值。方法区别于 OCR jailbreak 和像素级对抗扰动，重点不是让模型“看错”，而是让模型在正确解析复杂结构时把注意力和推理预算更多分配给任务完成，从而削弱安全提示和拒答方向的作用。","experiments":"实验覆盖六个主流 MLLM，包括文中提到的 GPT-5 和 Gemini 2.5，并构造十类威胁场景。StructBreak 的平均攻击成功率为 92.0%，峰值达到 97%，高于文中比较的既有攻击方法。一个有信息量的发现是，图结构推理能力更强的模型在 VKG 攻击下反而更容易失守。机制分析部分显示，高结构负载会导致注意力分布更分散，对系统安全提示的注意力被压低。潜空间几何分析还显示，StructBreak 输入相对标准有害提示落在异常分布区域，并且与模型的 refusal direction 接近正交，解释了为什么常规拒答机制难以覆盖这类输入。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中 SCO 假设的定义、Threat Model and Methodology 中 VKG 生成和意图解耦设计、实验表格中的 ASR 对比，以及机制分析里关于 attention dissipation 和 refusal direction 的图。无需深读攻击模板细节，除非关注多模态安全评测或对齐数据构造。需要保留一点审慎：文中将注意力变化解释为安全注意力消散，但注意力本身不是完整因果证据；更强的结论需要干预实验或训练时消融支持。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a safety failure mode in MLLMs under high structural load. When a model has to parse visual knowledge graphs and multi-hop structural relations, the reasoning burden can weaken refusal behavior and lead to harmful compliance.","importance":"The main value for pretraining researchers is diagnostic rather than algorithmic. It exposes a gap in multimodal safety evaluation: stronger structural reasoning does not automatically imply stronger safety. Alignment pipelines that focus on textual refusal patterns, OCR attacks, or pixel-level perturbations may miss failures induced by complex graph-like inputs. This matters for native multimodal pretraining and unified image-text modeling, where structural reasoning ability is often an explicit target.","technical_approach":"StructBreak is a black-box image-text attack framework. It uses Visual Knowledge Graphs as the carrier of malicious intent while keeping the textual instruction relatively benign. The malicious request is decomposed into graph nodes, edges, and reasoning paths, while the prompt is framed as tasks such as academic analysis, debugging, or structure interpretation. The pipeline generates adversarial VKGs, filters them, and adaptively adjusts structural complexity until the model crosses a safety failure threshold. The distinction from OCR jailbreaks or adversarial noise is important: the attack does not rely on misperception. It relies on correct structural parsing consuming enough reasoning and attention budget that safety behavior becomes less effective.","experiments":"The evaluation covers six leading MLLMs, including GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5, across ten threat scenarios. recipe、模型尺寸收益、post-training 效果，都会用公开 leaderboard 分数做判断。如果分数中混入了 benchmark 局部依赖、提交来源、部署配置和残差结构，那么直接用总分比较模型或拟合 scaling law 会高估某些干预的作用，也可能错判能力之间的关系。论文给出的测量框架提醒研究者：评测分数不是干净标签，尤其不适合未经建模就拿来解释训练改动。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一，用 Confirmatory Factor Analysis 和结构方程模型检验 benchmark item 背后的潜变量结构。论文发现 bifactor leaderboard scores actually measure. It focuses on how much of model ranking variation comes from latent capability versus benchmark structure, item dependence, contributor provenance, architecture, deployment choices, and statistical noise. The core issue is that aggregate scores are widely used as if they were clean measurements, while the measurement assumptions are rarely tested.","importance":"The paper is most relevant as an evaluation and scaling-law caution. Pretraining decisions are often justified with leaderboard deltas, size-performance curves, and comparisons between training interventions. If aggregate scores mix true capability with benchmark residuals, provenance effects, and deployment artifacts, then naive comparisons can misattribute gains. The practical message is clear: leaderboard scores should not be treated as clean labels for model capability without modeling their measurement structure.","technical_approach":"The framework has three layers. First, the authors use Confirmatory Factor Analysis and structural equation modeling to test latent structures behind benchmark item responses. A bifactor structure is identified: a general capability factor coexists with benchmark-specific factors, while residual dependencies remain. Second, they use Generalizability Theory with mixed-effects variance decomposition to estimate how much score variation is associated with facets such as architecture, benchmark, contributor or provenance, and deployment type. Third, they run mixed-effects latent regression, relating latent capabilities to model size and post-training practices while controlling for the structure and noise identified in the first two stages. This is a more disciplined alternative to regressing raw leaderboard averages on parameter count.","experiments":"The study analyzes more than 4,000 models from the Open LLM Leaderboard. The main findings are that commonly assumed reporting structures underestimate relationships among benchmarks, leaderboard items show local dependence, and ecosystem metadata explains a measurable fraction of ranking variation. Contributor metadata accounts for roughly 9% of score variance, while model-size-linked variance is reported at around 40%. The paper also finds that ranking reliability can be higher for contributor metadata than for architecture or deployment type. In the latent regressions, scaling generally improves performance, but not uniformly: progress on one benchmark-specific construct can be negatively associated with performance on another. The authors provide denoised scaling curves for general capability and benchmark-specific constructs, and analyze size and post-training effects after noise controls.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30 minutes. Auditing such contamination is challenging in time series because signals are continuous and heterogeneous, and often lack corpus documentation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study pretraining contamination auditing for TSFMs. We formalize the problem","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-24T14:59:12+00:00","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26161.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究的是时间序列 foundation model 的预训练污染审计。具体说，不是问某个样本是否被记住，而是问一个评测数据集是否在预训练语料中以原始、缩放、重命名、重窗口化等形式出现过。时间序列和文本不同，连续值、异构频率、预处理变体很多，简单的字符串重合、近重复检索、静态 loss 阈值或标准 membership inference 都不太可靠。论文要解决的是：在拿不到完整预训练语料文档、也不可能重训大量 shadow model 的现实条件下，如何对 TSFM 的 benchmark contamination 做可操作的审计。","importance":"它不属于 LLM 基座主线，但和预训练研究里的 contamination auditing、benchmark validity、membership inference 有明显方法学关联。启发点主要有两层。第一，论文把“静态低 loss 不等于污染”讲得很清楚：时间序列里平滑、强周期、低熵数据即使没见过，也可能天然容易预测，因此单看 perplexity 风格指标会误报。第二，它把审计信号从静态拟合优度转到 probe adaptation dynamics，也就是看模型在小规模微调探针下的学习速度和参数位移。这种思路对其他模态的预训练污染检测也有借鉴意义，尤其是在内容重合难定义、语料谱系不透明的场景里。贡献量级偏方法型和评测型，不是基础范式变化。","technical_approach":"TSFMAudit 的核心假设是：如果某个审计数据集在预训练中被见过或高度近似见过，那么模型在这个数据集上的少量 probe fine-tuning 会表现出异常高效的适应动态。这里的“高效”不是只指初始 loss 低，而是指在很少更新步数下 loss 降得更快，同时 backbone 参数移动更小。直觉上，污染数据离模型已有表示更近，因此只需要轻微调整就能对齐到更低误差。\n\n基于这个观察，方法流程大致分三步。第一，对候选 TSFM 和待审计数据集做一个受控的 probe fine-tuning，训练预算很小，目的是激发可比较的适应动态，而不是把任务真正训好。第二，从这个短程适应过程中提取动态特征，包括 loss reduction、参数 displacement，以及文中提到的其他 probe-time signals。第三，用这些特征构造 contamination risk score，判断该数据集是否可能出现在预训练语料中。\n\n论文的一个重要设计是把审计目标定义在 dataset level，而不是单样本 level。原因很现实：时间序列 benchmark 往往由同一底层 source series 经过窗口切分、缩放、重采样得到多个样本，单样本判断既不稳定，也不符合实际 benchmark 污染的关切。作者因此先形式化了“样本来自某个底层 source time series，而该 source 是否在预训练语料中出现过”的定义，再把污染程度写成 dataset-level contamination ratio。\n\n和 LLM 文献里的几类基线相比，TSFMAudit 刻意避开了两个难点。它不依赖显式 overlap detection，因为时间序列的近重复常常不是字面重复；也不依赖 shadow model 大规模校准 membership score，因为重训 TSFM 成本太高。它选择 probe dynamics，是因为这类信号同时利用了模型已有记忆和局部可塑性，能比静态 loss 更好地区分“天然容易预测”和“曾经见过”。","experiments":"实验覆盖 6 个 TSFM，使用各模型公开报告的预训练语料信息，并在 GIFT-Eval 和 TIME benchmark 上做审计，总计 187 个数据集。比较对象包括从 LLM 污染检测文献改造来的 10 个基线。论文给出的主结论是：TSFMAudit 在不同模型和 benchmark 上都更稳定，检测性能 consistently stronger。\n\n从引言和方法描述能看出的关键实验点有几项。第一，作者展示了污染数据集在 probe fine-tuning 下通常有更快的 loss reduction 和更小的 backbone movement，这个现象既是方法动机，也是后续特征设计的依据。第二，特征消融表明 adaptation dynamics 确实比单纯静态 loss 更有判别力，尤其参数位移这类“模型为了适应数据需要动多少”的信号是有增益的。第三，论文强调其方法适用于多种 TSFM，而不是绑定某个特定架构。\n\n给出的全文片段没有完整结果表和具体 AUC / F1 数字，因此不宜编造精确分数。但实验规模、比较设置和结论方向是明确的：作者不是做个案分析，而是在较大范围 benchmark 上验证 probe dynamics 作为 contamination signal 的有效性。","reading_guide":"如果你主要关心预训练污染审计的方法迁移，先看 Introduction 和 Problem Formulation。这里把为什么 LLM 里的 overlap / perplexity / membership inference 不能直接搬到时间序列讲得比较透，也把 dataset-level contamination 的定义立住了。\n\n然后看方法部分里 probe fine-tuning 的设置、提取了哪些动态特征、最终如何组合成风险分数。这里最值得想的是：哪些特征反映“记忆”，哪些特征反映“可塑性”，以及两者为什么能区分污染与天然易预测数据。\n\n实验部分优先看 feature ablation 和跨模型泛化结果。若只做泛读，30 分钟左右足够；若你做 benchmark hygiene、membership inference 或多模态 foundation model 审计，建议把附录里的实现细节和数据构造也看一下。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies pretraining contamination auditing for time-series foundation models. The question is not whether a single sample was memorized, but whether an evaluation dataset may have appeared in pretraining, possibly under rescaling, renaming, rewindowing, or other preprocessing changes. This is harder than in text because time series are continuous, heterogeneous, and often poorly documented at the corpus level. Straightforward overlap checks, static loss thresholds, or standard membership inference do not transfer cleanly. The paper asks how to build a practical contamination auditor without full corpus provenance and without retraining many shadow models.","importance":"This is outside core LLM pretraining, but it is methodologically relevant to contamination auditing, benchmark validity, and membership inference. The main lesson is that low static loss is not enough evidence of contamination in time series: smooth or strongly seasonal series can be easy to predict even when unseen. The paper therefore shifts the signal from static fit quality to probe-time adaptation dynamics. That idea may transfer to other modalities where exact overlap is ill-defined and corpus lineage is opaque. The contribution is mainly methodological and evaluative rather than a change in foundation-model training itself.","technical_approach":"$13","experiments":"$14","reading_guide":"If your main interest is contamination auditing methodology, start with the Introduction and Problem Formulation. Those sections explain clearly why overlap checks, perplexity-style tests, and standard membership inference from the LLM literature do not directly transfer to time series, and they establish the dataset-level contamination objective.\n\nThen read the method section with attention to the probe fine-tuning setup, the dynamic features that are extracted, and how they are combined into a risk score. The most useful question to keep in mind is which features reflect prior memory and which reflect plasticity, and why that combination separates contamination from naturally easy data.\n\nIn the experiments, prioritize the feature ablations and the cross-model results. We close one theory-benchmark loop on this primitive: a finite-envelope proposition tying a measurable pairwise-disagreement rate to whether the strict ordering admits a configuration-pair reversal, paired with a commit-stamped evaluation protocol that operationalises it on widely cited alignment benchmarks. On ever","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T06:54:44+00:00","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25492.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“模型谁更安全”，而是“安全 benchmark 给出的模型排序到底有多稳”。作者抓住一个很具体、也很常被忽略的问题：在对齐/安全评测里，prompt 模板、few-shot 数、解码设置、评分规则、量化方式、评测 harness 的实现细节，往往没有被完整规定，但这些选择本身就可能改变模型间的相对排名。论文要解决的是，如何把这种由评测配置引入的排序不稳定性，定义成一个可测、可复现实验对象，而不是停留在泛泛而谈的“benchmark 有噪声”。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接意义不在于提出新的训练方法，而在于提醒：很多被拿来比较基座模型、对齐策略或数据配方的安全结论，可能并不稳固。尤其当研究者把 TruthfulQA、BBQ、ToxiGen、XSTest 这类分数当作模型能力或对齐质量的证据时，如果评测配置本身能翻转 pairwise verdict，那么这些 benchmark 对训练决策的反馈就带有较强方法方差。对做 pretrain/post-train 接口研究的人，这篇文章的价值在于把“评测协议是训练闭环的一部分”说清楚了：如果评测对象没有被严格定义，训练优化的目标也会漂移。贡献量级偏方法论和测量学，不是新 benchmark，也不是新模型，但对读论文和做实验的口径控制有现实影响。","technical_approach":"作者的核心设计有两层。第一层是定义一个配置条件下的 pairwise-disagreement / rank-flip 指标。不是看单个分数的标准差，而是直接看“在一个事先承诺、commit-stamped 的配置包络内，模型 A 是否可能从优于 B 变成劣于 B”。这个指标更贴近实际使用场景，因为外部读者通常看到的是‘A 比 B 好/安全’，而不是方差分解表。第二层是把这个包络做成分层的、实践导向的配置网格：core tier 近似主流 benchmark/harness 默认设置，stress tier 再加入更激进但仍合理的设置，比如 T4 CoT、T=0.7 解码等。全文的实验主轴是在单一 harness 内，对 3 个 7–9B 开源 instruct 模型、5 个 alignment benchmark、612 个配置单元做穷举式测量，然后统计每个 benchmark 上 pairwise verdict 被配置翻转的比例。作者还用 LightGBM + SHAP 做轴级归因，分析哪些配置维度更主导 rank flip。除此之外，论文单独做了一个跨评测框架案例：在名义上相同的 anchor 配置下，把同一模型送进 lm-eval-harness、HELM-lite、Inspect AI，发现分数差异很大。这里作者刻意不把它解释成单轴“框架误差”，而是指出三者评测的 candidate object 实际并不等价，例如 TruthfulQA 的打分对象分别可能是 canonical answer set 的 logprob、concat-then-rank 的 joint multiple choice、或 A/B/C/D letter 的 logprob。这个处理比较克制，也说明作者关心的是测量对象定义，而不只是软件 bug。","experiments":"实验范围刻意收窄在消费级 16GB 显存、NF4 量化部署、3 个 7–9B 开源 instruct 模型、5 个安全/对齐 benchmark、固定题目子集。作者明确说不把结论外推成“所有 LLM 评测都不可靠”。在这个范围内，结果很强：所有被测 benchmark 上，单靠配置选择就可以翻转 pairwise verdict。XSTest 最极端，Qwen、Mistral、Yi 三个模型的 6 种两两排序都能在配置包络内实现。方差分解方面，在 BBQ、ToxiGen、XSTest 这几个 free-form benchmark 上，实现/配置主效应解释的总体方差至少与模型身份相当；只有 TruthfulQA 更明显由模型主导。与此同时，model-by-implementation interaction 普遍很大，说明不存在一个对所有模型都“等比例偏移”的简单校正项。跨框架案例里，在 bf16、greedy、0-shot、300 examples、seed 42 的名义 anchor 配置下，仅更换评测包，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 在 TruthfulQA 上的 reported score 跨度达到 21.7 个百分点，在 BBQ 上达到 22.7 个百分点。作者还做了一个保守的小规模 scale probe，试图排除“只是某个模型家族特殊”的混淆，但没有把它包装成 scaling law 结论。对 skim 级阅读，最该记住的数字就是：612-cell grid、所有 benchmark 都出现 rank flip、XSTest 上 6 种排序全可达、跨框架分数差可到 20+ 个点。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 和 §5.1，把作者到底在测什么先钉牢：不是分数波动，而是 pairwise verdict 是否可翻转。然后看配置包络的定义，区分 core tier 和 stress tier，理解作者为何强调 commit-stamped 和 practice-derived。接着看方差分解与 rank-flip 结果，尤其是 XSTest 的排序可达性，以及 TruthfulQA 与其他 benchmark 的差异。再看 SHAP 归因，判断主要不稳定性来自 prompt、scoring、decoding 也看一遍。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not asking which model is safer. It asks whether safety/alignment benchmarks provide stable pairwise rankings at all. The concrete issue is that benchmark papers often leave key evaluator choices under-specified: prompt template, decoding setup, few-shot count, scoring rule, quantization regime, and even the exact harness implementation. Those choices can change the relative ordering between models. The paper turns that concern into a measurable object: configuration-conditional rank instability, rather than a vague statement that benchmarks are noisy.","importance":"Its value for pretraining researchers is indirect but real. Safety and alignment benchmarks are often used as evidence when comparing base models, post-training recipes, or data mixtures. If the pairwise verdict itself can flip under plausible evaluator configurations, then the benchmark signal fed back into model development is less reliable than the headline numbers suggest. This matters at the pretrain↔post-train interface: if the evaluation target is not tightly specified, optimization pressure can drift toward artifacts of the harness rather than properties of the model. The contribution is methodological rather than algorithmic. It does not introduce a new benchmark or a new model, but it sharpens how benchmark evidence should be interpreted.","technical_approach":"$15","experiments":"$16","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the section defining the rank-flip metric. The key is to understand that the target quantity is not score variance but the existence of pairwise reversals under a declared configuration envelope. Then read the envelope construction carefully, especially the distinction between the core tier and the stress tier, because much of the paper’s credibility depends on that choice. After that, focus on the main rank-flip results and the variance decomposition, especially why TruthfulQA behaves differently from BBQ, ToxiGen, and XSTest. The SHAP analysis is worth a quick pass to see which evaluator axes dominate instability. Finally, read the appendix case study on lm-eval-harness, HELM-lite, and Inspect AI. The main lesson there is not which package is better, but that nominally similar tasks may correspond to different scored objects. Around 25–35 minutes is enough for a solid skim; read the disclosure template as well if you run benchmark-heavy experiments.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"对齐评测可靠性的方法论文章，对做SFT/RLHF评估的研究者有直接参考——任何成对安全排名声称都应先通过SafetyRepro类方法检验配置稳定性","en":"Methodology paper on alignment evaluation reliability. Existing evaluations usually test only visual quality, navigation, memory, or a narrow task setting, making it hard to localize failures across rendering, world initialization, interaction following, state consistency, and physics.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is mainly evaluative rather than algorithmic. As video world models move toward controllable generation, embodied simulation, and open-ended environments, training progress will depend on signals beyond frame realism. WBench separates visual fidelity from controllability and persistent world state, which is useful for researchers working on native multimodal pretraining or video-based world modeling.","technical_approach":"Each case is decomposed into a World Setting and an Interaction sequence. The World Setting specifies scene, style, perspective, and subject, while the Interaction sequence defines multi-turn user controls. The benchmark covers four interaction types: navigation, subject action, event editing, and perspective switching. Navigation is represented through aligned text, camera-pose, and discrete-action interfaces, allowing models with different control APIs to be compared on a shared subset. Evaluation is organized around five dimensions: video quality, setting adherence, interaction adherence, consistency, and physics compliance, measured with 22 automatic sub-metrics using specialist vision models and VLM-based judges. The main design choice is to separate what the world is from what the user asks it to do, which makes failures easier to diagnose than in single-prompt video generation benchmarks.","experiments":"WBench contains 289 test cases and 1,058 interaction turns. Initial frames are generated with Nano Banana 2 and GPT-Image-1.5, supplemented by web-collected and manually captured images, followed by manual quality control. The paper evaluates 20 models. All models are compared on a shared navigation subset of 158 cases, while text-prompted image-to-video models are also evaluated on the full benchmark. The main empirical findings are that no model dominates all five dimensions; navigation is largely independent of the other abilities; camera control and perspective consistency are distinct; physics compliance correlates more with rendering quality than with control; difficulty varies by perspective, scene type, and subject; and multi-turn degradation is uneven across interaction types, with navigation being especially fragile.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Yet current systems operate over only narrow slices of that world, limiting context-sensitive reasoning and effective assistance. Existing benchmarks similarly provide only partial user state and therefore fail to capture performance in such a broad, always-on setting. agent","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26086.png","hf_upvotes":22,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 always-on personal assistant 的评测失真问题。现有 benchmark 往往只给 agent 一个狭窄、静态、局部的用户状态切片，因此模型看起来能做任务，但并没有真正面对“长期历史、多服务依赖、多设备交互”这些现实约束。Claw-Anything 想回答的是：如果把 agent 放进更接近真实数字生活的环境里，要求它利用长期事件流、跨后端服务状态、GUI/CLI 混合接口去完成 reactive 和 proactive 任务，现有模型到底还剩多少能力。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关系不强，但对 agent 训练数据和后训练评测有现实意义。它的重要性主要在两层。第一，它把 agent 的“可访问数字世界范围”明确提出为核心变量，而不是只看模型推理本身。这会影响后续很多训练设计：如果训练环境没有长期状态和跨系统依赖，模型学到的只是短程 scripted behavior。第二，它不只是 benchmark，还提供了可扩展的数据生成管线，并展示用这些环境生成成功轨迹去微调模型能带来明显提升。对做 agent mid-train、trajectory SFT、environment simulation 的人，这篇有方法资产价值；对纯 LLM pretrain 则更多是边界相关工作。","technical_approach":"方法核心是构建一个更宽上下文范围的 agent benchmark。作者把扩展分成三维：长时程活动历史、相互依赖的后端服务、以及多设备上的 GUI/CLI 交互。与很多现有环境只暴露单应用或单轮任务不同，这里 agent 需要在一个持续演化的 persona-grounded digital world 中行动。\n\n环境构建采用自动化合成管线。起点是一个最小 persona seed，然后用 LLM-based simulator 做 multi-round event injection，逐轮向用户世界中注入日常事件，并同步更新 persistent world state 和各类动态服务痕迹，比如邮件、日历、社交平台等。随着轮次增加，历史会累积，persona 会被逐步具体化，环境中也会出现噪声、无关事件甚至相互矛盾的信息。最后把“下一事件”实例化成一个 grounded task，并配一个 executable verifier，于是评测变成：agent 是否能在这个不断演化的数字生活里完成下一步合适动作。\n\n这个设计有两个值得注意的点。其一，任务不是脱离环境单独写出来的，而是从环境演化过程中自然长出来，因此更容易包含跨时间依赖和隐含前提。其二，作者把 proactive assistance 也纳入评测，要求模型不是只响应显式指令，而是从上下文中推断用户可能需要什么。这比传统 benchmark 更接近 always-on assistant 的真实目标。","experiments":"主实验结论很直接：在 Claw-Anything 上，GPT-5.5 的 pass@1 只有 34.5%，明显低于很多模型在既有 benchmark 上的表现。作者据此认为，现有 benchmark 低估了长期状态整合、跨服务协调和多设备操作带来的难度。这个结论对评测设计是成立的，因为 benchmark 的核心价值就在于暴露旧基准覆盖不到的失败模式。\n\n数据规模方面，论文构建了 200 个 human-verified evaluation tasks，以及 2,000 个 training environments。更关键的是，它验证了这些训练环境不只是“能生成数据”，而是能转化成有效监督：用从其中得到的 1,500 条 successful trajectories 去微调 Qwen3.5-27B，成功率提升 23.7%。这个数字说明环境模拟和任务合成至少在 agent 后训练上是有用的。\n\n从研究者视角，最值得关注的不是谁在榜单上第一，而是性能掉得有多厉害，以及微调后能回升多少。前者说明现有 agent 能力对环境复杂度很敏感；后者说明数据缺口可能比架构缺口更大，至少在这一类任务上如此。","reading_guide":"这篇按 skim 读即可。先看 难度来自哪里：是长历史、跨服务依赖，还是多设备交互。\n\n如果只有 15–20 分钟，建议读法是：引言 → benchmark 构造方法 → 主结果表 → 微调实验。若要多花一点时间，优先看 human verification 和 executable verifier 的设计，因为 benchmark 是否可信，主要取决于任务是否真的可执行、可判定，而不是环境描述写得多复杂。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses an evaluation mismatch for always-on personal assistants. Most existing benchmarks expose only narrow, static slices of user state, so agents are tested in settings that miss the core difficulties of real assistance: long activity histories, cross-service dependencies, and interaction across multiple devices and interfaces. Yet growing evidence suggests that these traces often fail to faithfully represent the computations behind a model's predictions. Several faithfulness metrics have been proposed, but whether they indeed measure faithfulness remains unknown. Answering this requires ground-truth labels, which are hard to obtain since internal computations are not directly observable. Consequently, most wor","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-05-24T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25052.png","hf_upvotes":13,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"链式思维（CoT）被广泛用作“模型在想什么”的证据，但大量工作表明 CoT 可能是事后编造。问题在于：已经提出的各种“faithfulness metric”（衡量 CoT 是否忠实于模型内部计算的指标）到底有没有在测 faithfulness？缺少 ground-truth 标签使得以往评估常用 plausibility/importance 等代理信号，容易把“看起来合理”误当成“真实反映计算”。论文要解决的是：构造可自动标注的、带 ground-truth faithfulness 标签的基准，用来直接 meta-evaluate 这些指标。","importance":"对预训练研究的影响是间接但现实：\n- 如果把 CoT 当作训练信号（R1 类蒸馏、过程监督、reasoning trace 合成、verifier/critic 训练），就需要知道“哪些 CoT 更可信”。这篇工作表明现有常用指标大多接近随机，意味着用这些指标筛选/加权 CoT 数据可能引入系统性偏差。\n- 对评测与对齐也有启发：很多监控/审计方案依赖 CoT 的可解释性假设，而该假设缺少可靠度量。\n整体上它属于 eval/interpretability 方向的基础设施工作，不直接改进预训练 recipe，但会影响“用什么过程数据、如何过滤过程数据”。","technical_approach":"贡献分三层：\n1) 定义层：把经典解释方法里的 faithfulness 定义（忠实反映导致预测的计算过程）扩展到“生成式 CoT”场景，因为现代 reasoning model 的 CoT 可能包含与最终答案弱相关的探索性步骤，不能简单套用静态解释的定义。\n2) 标注方法层：提出一种获得 ground-truth faithfulness 标签的任务设计原则：构造一些任务，使得模型若输出某个答案，必然执行过某些可识别的中间计算（例如必须遵循误导性 hint，或由题目结构强制某些中间结论），并且通过设计避免猜测/捷径绕过这些计算。于是可以检查 CoT 是否承认/体现这些必经计算，从而给 CoT-level 与 step-level 打“faithful/unfaithful”标签。\n3) 基准与评测层：构建 BonaFide 数据集与自动化标注流水线，并用它系统评估现有 faithfulness metrics（重要性类、语义效用类等），同时比较它们的计算成本与偏置（倾向把大多数样本判为 faithful 或 unfaithful）。","experiments":"关键实验结论在引言中给了较明确的数字：\n- 数据规模：BonaFide 含 1,120 条 CoT 与 1,946 个 CoT steps，覆盖 13 个任务；来自 10 个 4B–70B 的模型家族（Qwen3、OLMo 3、DeepSeek-R1-Distill、Llama 3.3 等，含 reasoning 与 non-reasoning 变体）。\n- 自动标注质量：与人工对照验证 precision 98.9%。\n- 指标有效性：大多数 faithfulness metric 接近随机；最强指标在不同粒度不一致——CC-SHAP 在 CoT-level 达到 0.70 AUROC，但在 step-level 不可靠；Filler Tokens 在 step-level 约 0.59 AUROC。\n- 系统性偏置：重要性类指标在平衡样本上把 90%–96% 的 CoT 判为 unfaithful；语义效用类把 94%–96% 判为 faithful，说明它们更像在测“某种相关属性”而非 faithfulness。\n- 计算成本：CC-SHAP 在长 CoT 上可达 10^3 秒/样本量级，难以用于在线监控或大规模数据过滤。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 45–60 分钟）：\n- 先读 §2（Revising chain-of-thought faithfulness）：把它如何区分 faithfulness / plausibility / importance 理清，这是理解后面“为什么现有指标会失效”的前提。\n- 重点读数据构造与自动标注流水线那一节：任务如何设计来“锁定必经计算”、如何防止 shortcut、step-level 标签如何定义与抽取。\n- 看评测结果表：不同 metric 在 CoT-level 与 step-level 的 AUROC、以及预测偏置分布；再看成本分析（哪些方法不可用）。\n- 如果关心训练数据过滤：把它的失败案例当作 checklist，思考在 CoT 合成/蒸馏管线里哪些启发式筛选其实不可靠。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Chains of thought are widely treated as evidence of what an LLM “actually did,” yet they can be post-hoc rationalizations. Many faithfulness metrics have been proposed, but it has been unclear whether they truly measure faithfulness because ground-truth labels are missing. The paper’s goal is to create tasks where certain intermediate computations are logically necessary for a given output, enabling automated ground-truth faithfulness labels for CoTs and individual steps, and then meta-evaluate existing metrics against those labels.","importance":"The impact on pretraining is indirect but practical. If you use CoTs as training signal—process supervision, R1-style distillation, synthetic reasoning traces, verifier/critic training, or any pipeline that filters/weights traces—you need a reliable notion of which traces are faithful. The paper shows most existing metrics are near chance and often heavily biased, implying that metric-based CoT filtering can systematically skew data. It also weakens monitoring/auditing approaches that assume CoTs are interpretable by default. Overall, it is evaluation infrastructure that informs how to treat reasoning traces as data.","technical_approach":"The work combines (1) a CoT-specific faithfulness definition, adapting classic explanation faithfulness to the setting where the model emits a long, potentially exploratory trace; (2) a labeling methodology based on task designs that make some intermediate computations unavoidable (e.g., misleading hints that must be followed to produce the observed answer, or intrinsic structure that forces specific intermediate results), while explicitly preventing guessing/shortcuts; and (3) the BonaFide benchmark plus an automated labeling pipeline that yields both CoT-level and step-level faithful/unfaithful labels, validated against human annotation. existin","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T09:23:19+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25621.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决流式全模态视频理解问题：模型在视频和音频连续到达时，需要在有限上下文预算内保留有用证据，并判断当前是否应该主动回应。","importance":"这篇论文更接近 multimodal pretraining 的影响在于：训练目标和数据格式可能需要包含等待、证据出现、证据不足这些状态，而不是只做离线 QA。","technical_approach":"StreamOV 包含三块设计。第一是 multimodal evidence routing。模型同时利用 query-agnostic 的流式动态信息和 query-aware 的语义相关性，把证据分成 visual-only、audio-only、audio-visual aligned 三类线索。这样做的原因是，流式视频中的关键信息不总是来自同一模态：有些问题依赖画面，有些依赖语音，有些依赖音画同步关系。第二是 long-short term memory。近期片段保留更稠密的信息，历史片段则稀疏保存更有信息量的证据，在固定预算下避免上下文无限增长。第三是 MLLM-as-a-trigger。它不生成显式 silence token，也不使用外部 router，而是利用模型早期解码 hidden states 判断 Respond 或 Wait。这个取舍比较合理：silence token 会改变生成分布，外部 router 又可能缺少多模态推理能力；直接读出 MLLM router。若只关心预训练，可重点看数据格式、触发标签和多轮流式 QA 的生成流程，思考它们能否变成原生多模态模型的训练任务。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses streaming omni-video understanding: a model receives audio and video continuously, must maintain useful evidence under a bounded context budget, and must decide when it has enough information to respond.","importance":"This is mainly a VLM and omni-modal systems/evaluation paper rather than a pretraining-method paper. Its relevance to foundation-model research is in the interface it exposes. Long-horizon multimodal models cannot rely on simply appending frames, audio segments, and dialogue history. They need evidence selection, memory management, and an explicit notion of when not to answer. This has implications for native multimodal pretraining: training data and objectives may need to encode waiting, evidence arrival, and evidence absence, not only offline question answering.","technical_approach":"StreamOV has three main components. First, multimodal evidence routing combines query-agnostic stream dynamics with query-aware semantic relevance, then separates evidence into visual-only, audio-only, and audio-visual-aligned cues. This matters because streaming questions may depend on images, speech, or temporal alignment between the two. Second, a long-short term memory keeps recent observations densely while retaining older evidence sparsely under a fixed budget. This avoids unbounded context growth while preserving salient history. Third, an MLLM-as-a-trigger mechanism uses early decoding hidden states to predict Respond or Wait. This avoids explicit silence-token generation, which can perturb the pretrained generation distribution, and avoids external routers, which may not share the MLLM’s multimodal reasoning state.","experiments":"$19","reading_guide":"A 25–40 minute skim is appropriate. Existing mainstream training paradigms for spatial reasoning largely rely on outcome alignment or process imitation, lacking explicit constraints on the reasoning process, and therefore struggle to ensure genuine visual dependence and stable reasoning trajectories. tw","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T07:27:28+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25524.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注 VLM 空间推理中的一个老问题：模型即使答对，也不一定真的看图推理，更不一定有稳定的推理过程。作者把问题进一步拆成两个可诊断的过程缺陷。其一是 Spurious Grounding，模型在去掉图像后仍能给出相似 CoT，说明它可能主要依赖语言先验和数据偏置。其二是 Tail Instability，推理后段的不确定性反而上升，说明模型在接近答案时轨迹不稳。论文要解决的不是再做一个更大的空间数据集，而是如何把“过程可靠性”直接写进 RL 目标，而不是只优化最终答案正确率。","importance":"对预训练主线来说，这不是核心论文，但有两个启发点。第一，它把 reasoning 训练中的过程退化现象显式量化了，说明只用 outcome reward 做 RL 很容易学到捷径，这和文本推理、工具使用、甚至代码推理中的 reward hacking 是同类问题。第二，它提出的约束方式不是监督 CoT 文本本身，而是约束推理过程中的不确定性轨迹与反事实行为，这比单纯 imitation 更接近“训练动力学约束”。如果以后把类似思想迁移到纯文本 LM 或多模态统一模型的 post-train，可能会比直接奖励长 CoT 更稳。","technical_approach":"方法分两段。先做数据和诊断，再做过程塑形 RL。作者先构建了一个覆盖多类空间现象的 CoT 数据集，用它对模型做 SFT，得到具备初始空间 CoT 能力的 VLM。然后在这个基础上用 vanilla GRPO 做 RL，并系统分析 rollout 行为，发现仅靠最终答案奖励会诱发两类退化：Spurious Grounding 和 Tail Instability。\n\n围绕这两个问题，ProSR 在 GRPO 目标里加入两个过程约束。第一个是 Counterfactual Invariance Penalty，用来抑制伪视觉依赖。具体做法是在 rollout 时额外引入 blank-image 条件，比较真实图像与空白图像条件下的 normalized entropy trajectory。如果两种条件下的不确定性轨迹过于相似，就施加惩罚。直觉上，真正依赖视觉证据的推理过程，在去掉图像后应当明显改变；如果几乎不变，说明模型没有认真用视觉输入。\n\n第二个是 Tail Drift Penalty，用来抑制推理后段的不稳定。作者观察到合理的推理过程中，不确定性应当随着证据积累逐步收敛；若在后半段再次抬升，往往意味着模型在临近答案时发生摇摆、补编理由或切换策略。因此他们对 reasoning tail 的异常熵上升施加惩罚，鼓励轨迹在接近答案时稳定收束。\n\n这个方法和常见 process supervision 的区别在于，它不直接要求模型模仿某条标准 CoT，而是把“是否看图”和“是否稳定收敛”变成可优化的行为约束。这样做的好处是，约束的是过程性质，而不是某种固定表述模板，理论上更有利于泛化。","experiments":"作者在多个复杂和 OOD 空间推理 benchmark 上评测，报告平均准确率比已有方法提升 3.7%。除了答案准确率，论文还专门设计了四个诊断指标来测过程质量：Blank-image Accuracy、Same-Answer Rate、Normalized Trajectory Similarity、Late-Rise Rate。前几项主要测 Spurious Grounding，最后一项主要测 Tail Instability。\n\n实验结论有两层。第一，ProSR 在常规 benchmark 上确实提升了最终任务表现，说明过程约束没有和答案正确率冲突。第二，更关键的是诊断指标同步改善：blank-image 条件下的异常可答性下降，真实图像与空白图像下的轨迹相似度降低，后段不确定性异常上升的比例也下降。这说明模型不是单纯学会了另一种表面模式，而是在更大程度上依赖视觉证据，并且推理轨迹更稳定。\n\n从论文叙述看，作者还做了 vanilla GRPO 与 ProSR 的对比，以及两个 penalty 的消融。预期结论是两个约束各自对应不同退化模式，联合使用效果最好。给出的全文片段没有展开模型规模、训练步数、数据量和每个 benchmark 的逐项数字，因此无法在这里复述更细的表格，但实验设计本身是完整的：先证明问题存在，再证明约束能定向修复问题。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看引言里的问题定义和 Fig.1，确认作者发现的两类退化是否有说服力。然后直接看方法部分关于两个 penalty 的公式和实现，重点理解为什么用 entropy trajectory，而不是直接比较 token 或 CoT 文本。这个选择决定了方法是否只是另一个 process reward engineering。\n\n接着看诊断指标定义和主实验表。若时间有限，优先看 blank-image 相关指标和 late-rise 指标，因为这两组最能说明方法到底有没有改变推理机制。最后看消融，判断 Counterfactual Invariance Penalty 与 Tail Drift Penalty 是否各自解决了对应问题，还是只是共同提供了正则化。\n\n预计 30 到 45 分钟可以完成有效阅读。更值得带着问题去看的点有两个：一是 blank-image 反事实是否会误伤某些强语言先验任务；二是 entropy trajectory 作为过程代理变量是否足够稳健，是否会被解码策略或温度设置影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a familiar weakness in VLM spatial reasoning: a model can produce the correct answer without genuinely grounding its reasoning in the image, and even when it does reason, the trajectory may be unstable. The authors make this concrete with two diagnosed failure modes. Spurious Grounding means the model produces similar CoT behavior even when the image is removed, suggesting reliance on language priors or dataset bias. Tail Instability means uncertainty rises again late in the reasoning chain, indicating unstable convergence near answer generation. The goal is therefore not just better spatial accuracy, but process-level reliability under RL training.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is relevant to anyone thinking about reasoning optimization. The main lesson is that answer-only RL objectives can induce shortcut behavior even when benchmark accuracy improves. That is directly related to reward hacking in text reasoning, tool use, and code generation. A second useful idea is methodological: instead of supervising a canonical CoT string, the paper constrains process properties through uncertainty trajectories and counterfactual behavior. That is closer to shaping training dynamics than to plain imitation, and the idea could plausibly transfer beyond spatial VLMs.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"$1b","reading_guide":"$1c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"可扫数据诊断和两个 penalty；若证据扎实，它会提醒 VLM RL 不应只盯 outcome reward。","en":"Skim the diagnostics and the two penalties; if the evidence is clean, it argues against optimizing VLM RL on outcome reward alone."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jiangyang Li","Cong Wan","Changjie Wu","Songlin Dong","Lingjun Zhang","Linzhe Shi","Xu Wang","Zhiheng Ma","Hang Zhang","Mu Xu","Yihong Gong"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["vlm","spatial-reasoning","process-supervision","reinforcement-learning","chain-of-thought"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25524","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.25524","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.25343","title":"Toward Native Multimodal Modeling: A Roadmap","abstract":"Multimodal modeling represents a vital step from modality-agnostic reasoning toward world modeling. While early approaches predominantly rely on late-fusion that assembles encoders and frozen language backbones with output heads, recent efforts have shifted the paradigm toward native multimodal modeling (NMM) with the intrinsic integration of modalities for superior multimodal performance. Despite its potential, the design space of native architectures remains insufficiently defined. In this pap","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T01:57:43+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25343.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章要解决的问题不是提出一个新的 native multimodal 模型，而是把“什么叫 native（原生）多模态建模”这件事形式化：如何区分 late-fusion（外挂式拼装）、mid-fusion（中层注入/交互）与 early-fusion（统一 token 空间/统一骨干），以及如何用一套可操作的维度去描述现有模型在“架构融合深度、输入输出对称性、训练目标与 early-fusion，避免把不同范式的经验混用；\n2) 把输入-输出的对偶（multi-to-text vs multi-to-multi symmetric）作为主轴之一，能更直接地映射到预训练目标设计（AR、mask/any-order、diffusion/AR 混合）与 tokenizer 选择。\n但它不是实证型 recipe paper：没有给出新的 scaling 两个原生区间与 non-native（late-fusion）做切分。\n2) 以 input-output duality 组织模型谱系：把现有系统按输入模态集合与输出模态集合分成 multi-to-text、multi-to-target（面向图像/语音/视频生成等）、multi-to-multi symmetric（理解与生成对称统一）等类型；这背后对应不同的 tokenization（离散/连续、patch/codec）、不同的解码器形态（纯 AR、AR+diffusion、双头/多头）与训练目标组合。\n3) 设计空间的关键维度梳理：围绕“统一 token 空间 vs 特征注入”“共享 backbone vs Unlike conventional vector quantization, which assigns a discrete token to each patch feature vector, CVQ quantizes each channel of the feature map. This formulation represents an image as discrete levels of visual details, rather than as a grid of spatial patches. Based on CVQ, we introduce a new visual autoregressive framework with \"next-channel predi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26089.png","hf_upvotes":13,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"传统 patch-wise VQ 把图像离散化为二维 patch token 网格，带来两类问题：一是 patch embedding 高度冗余导致 codebook 利用率低、dead codes/塌缩与重建信息损失；二是为了做 AR 生成不得不把 2D token 强行展平成 1D（raster/z-curve 等），序列顺序与图像依赖结构不匹配，导致“next-token”学习不自然。论文要解决的是：能否通过改变离散化轴，让图像 token 天然成为 1D 序列，同时缓解 codebook collapse，并形成更 AR-friendly 的生成顺序。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接价值不在“更好的图像生成指标”，而在视觉 tokenizer 设计空间：它提出一种与 patch token 正交的离散化范式（按 channel 量化），把“token 顺序”从外部手工排序变成由表示结构内生决定。对统一多模态 LM（把视觉当 token 序列喂给 AR LLM）尤其相关：如果 channel token 真能稳定提供更好的 codebook 使用与更合理的序列结构，它可能降低视觉离散化的工程复杂度（少依赖额外正则/技巧），并改变后续 AR/any-order/diffusion 的建模接口。不过从预训练角度，它更像 tokenizer/表示层创新，是否能在大规模图文预训练中带来稳定收益仍需要外部复现与更大规模验证。","technical_approach":"核心做法是把 VQ 的“分块单位”从空间位置 (i,j) 的 1×1×c 向量，改为沿通道维度的切片：对 latent feature map Z∈R^{h×w×c}，不再对每个空间点的 c 维向量做 nearest neighbor，而是对每个 channel（对应一个 h×w 的二维图）做量化，从而得到长度为 c 的 1D token 序列。直觉是：自编码器的不同通道往往承载不同层级的视觉信息（更全局的结构到更细的纹理/属性），按通道离散化会让每个 token 内部“携带全局空间结构”，序列天然是一维。\n\n为缓解传统 VQ 的 codebook collapse，论文给出机制解释：patch-wise 下不同图像的 patch embedding 在 batch 内高度重叠，早期训练会把大量 patch 聚到少数 code 上，更新集中导致其他 code 变“死码”。channel-wise 切分后，不同图像在 channel 维度的表示更可分，覆盖更广的 codebook，从优化动态上更不容易塌缩。\n\n在生成建模上，提出 Channel-wise Auto-Regressive (CAR)：把图像生成改写为“next-channel prediction”，按通道顺序逐个预测 token，使生成过程呈现从粗到细的 progressive enrichment。为了让通道序列顺序更适合 AR，使用 nested dropout 来诱导一个从“更重要/更基础信息”到“更细节信息”的通道排序（使前缀通道可先重建出草图式结构，后续通道补细节）。这相当于把图像的 coarse-to-fine 生成顺序从空间扫描改为表示维度扫描。","experiments":"给定的全文片段未包含完整实验表格与具体数值（如 codebook perplexity/usage、重建 PSNR/LPIPS、生成 FID/IS、模型规模与训练数据规模等），只能从方法动机推断其主要验证点应包括：\n1) codebook utilization：对比 patch-wise VQ 与 CVQ 的 code 使用分布、dead code 比例、训练早期的塌缩曲线；\n2) reconstruction：在相同码率/相同 codebook size 下的重建质量提升；\n3) AR generation：CAR 的 tokens, which (1) tends to under-utilize the codebook due to patch redundancy, causing dead codes and information loss in reconstruction, and (2) forces an unnatural 2D-to-1D flattening (raster/z-order) for autoregressive modeling, leading to a mismatch between token order and image dependencies. The paper asks whether changing the quantization axis can yield a genuinely 1D visual token sequence while improving codebook utilization and making AR generation more natural.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model pretraining is primarily as a tokenizer/representation contribution rather than a new training recipe. timestep；这些信息描述了模型实际生成答案时哪些位置、哪些时刻影响更大。第二类是 training feedback，来自去噪训练阶段的预测概率；作者只保留更有信息量的位置，避免把大量低价值或噪声较大的 token 都放进 policy update。基于这些反馈，RLDF 对 timestep 做加权采样，优先选择预测不确定性高、对最终生成更敏感的去噪步骤。损失不是在所有 denoising step 上计算，也不是用 no-masking 或 random-masking 粗略近似，而是在采样到的中间 noisy state 上优化模型预测 clipped clean state \\(\\hat{x}_0\\)。选择 clean state 而不是 next state 是一个重要设计：next-state 目标更贴近一步去噪转移，但容易放大局部误差和引入 loss spike；clean-state 目标把监督信号直接对齐到最终答案，更稳定，也更能利用 verifier reward 或 outcome reward。论文还开源 Drift，一个面向 dLLM 的模块化 RL 框架，用于承载这类算法。","experiments":"实验覆盖两个代表性 dLLM 架构：LLaDA 和 Dream。任务集中在 math 和 code In autoregressive models, sequence log probabilities can be obtained with a single causal forward pass. In dLLMs, generation is an iterative denoising process with bidirectional attention and flexible decoding order, making exact policy loss computation expensive and often intractable. RLDF aims to provide a more accurate and efficient estimator for RL training.","importance":"The paper is important for post-training diffusion language models. Models such as LLaDA and Dream have attractive modeling properties, including bidirectional attention, arbitrary decoding order, and potential multi-token parallel generation. Their reasoning training pipelines, however, are much less mature than those of autoregressive LLMs. RLDF is not just a reward modification. It reformulates how RL loss should be attached to the denoising trajectory. By reducing the mismatch between training-time masking patterns and inference-time denoising states, it offers a practical path toward scalable reasoning-oriented RL for dLLMs.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"The experiments cover two representative diffusion language model families, LLaDA and Dream, with evaluations on math and code reasoning tasks. Such uniform assignment ignores which steps contributed to success or failure. Improving credit assignment can address this limitation by enabling targeted refinement of faulty reasoning steps, rather than updating entire trajectories uniformly. pr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T07:11:50+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25507.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决 RLVR 训练长链推理时的信用分配问题。现有 GRPO 类方法通常把一个最终可验证奖励均匀传播到整条轨迹，导致正确步骤、错误步骤、无关步骤都被同等更新。对长 CoT 来说，这会稀释训练信号，也会让模型难以针对真正出错的位置改进。","importance":"这是对推理后训练方法的实质改进，也对预训练研究有间接价值。关键点是把“结果奖励”变成更局部的“从某个状态继续生成后的反事实结果差异”。这提供了一种不依赖人工过程标注的过程级训练信号。对做 RL pretrain、long reasoning data、annealing/posttrain 接口的人来说，价值在于训练信号质量：不是简单增加 rollout 数量，而是把 rollout 放在更可能改变成败的中间状态上。理论部分也给出清楚解释：如果可改进状态在当前策略下出现概率为 pπ，那么只在这些状态 reset 可以把样本复杂度改善一个 1/pπ² 量级，并把单轮策略提升放大 1/pπ。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 reset。先从失败推理轨迹中选一个中间状态，把它当成共享前缀，然后从这个状态重新采样多个 suffix continuation。由于这些续写共享相同前缀，它们最终成功或失败的差异更容易归因到 reset 点之后的决策。训练时只对 suffix tokens 做 policy-gradient 更新，而不是更新整条原始轨迹。RRPO 随机选择 reset 状态，提供一个简单基线。SRPO 则让模型自定位失败轨迹中的第一个错误步骤，然后从该位置 reset；它不需要外部 step-level label，利用模型已有的自我错误定位能力近似 credit-assignment oracle。理论上，作者用 Conservative Policy Iteration 构造两个版本：CPI-RR 从随机 reset 状态更新，CPI-CARO 假设有一个 oracle 能判断某状态是否存在 advantage 超过阈值 τ 的改进行动。这个 oracle 版本解释了为什么 reset 到“可改进状态”比随机 reset 更有效，也为 SRPO 的设计提供依据。","experiments":"实验覆盖十个推理 benchmark，包含数学、科学、策略和常识推理。SRPO 优于 GRPO，以及带自我修正或 shared-prefix continuation 的 contemporaneous RL 基线。在代码任务 LiveCodeBench 上，SRPO 收敛到更高 pass rate，并且相对 GRPO 和 RRPO 学习速度快 2–3 倍。消融的重点不只是最终分数，而是 reset 位置质量：更干净的 prefix 带来更高 correction rate，clean prefixes 的修正成功率接近 erroneous prefixes 的 2 倍。结果支持一个实际判断：reset 本身有用，但收益高度依赖错误定位质量；如果 reset 点选在已经被污染的前缀之后，后续采样组的学习信号会明显变差。","reading_guide":"建议精读。优先看 Introduction 中对 RLVR 信用分配问题的表述和 Figure 1；再看算法部分中 RRPO 与 SRPO 的 rollout 组织方式，尤其是只更新 suffix tokens 的细节；理论部分重点读 CPI-RR、CPI-CARO 和 Theorem 1，理解 pπ 因子如何进入样本复杂度与单轮改进；实验部分重点看十个 benchmark 总表、LiveCodeBench 曲线，以及 self-localization quality 的消融。值得深挖的问题有三个：第一，self-localization 错误会不会引入系统性负反馈；第二，reset suffix 更新和 KL 约束如何平衡，避免局部修正破坏整体推理风格；第三，这类方法能否前移到 mid-training 或高质量推理数据生成，而不只用于最终 RL 后训练。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets credit assignment in RLVR for long-form reasoning. Standard GRPO-style training uses a single verifiable outcome reward and applies it broadly across the whole trajectory. This makes correct, incorrect, and irrelevant reasoning steps receive nearly the same learning signal, which is especially inefficient for long chains of thought.","importance":"This is a meaningful step for reasoning post-training and a useful signal-design result for pretraining researchers. The central idea is to turn outcome reward into a more localized counterfactual signal: restart from an intermediate reasoning state, sample several continuations, and compare their outcomes. This gives a process-like learning signal without human step-level supervision. The theoretical result is also useful: if improvable states occur with on-policy probability pπ, concentrating resets on those states improves sample complexity by a factor of 1/pπ² and increases per-iteration improvement by 1/pπ compared with random resets.","technical_approach":"The shared primitive is reset-based rollout generation. Given a failed trajectory, the method selects an intermediate state as a shared prefix, samples multiple suffix continuations from that state, and applies policy-gradient updates only to the suffix tokens. Since the continuations share the same prefix, differences in final outcome can be attributed more directly to decisions after the reset point. RRPO chooses the reset state uniformly at random. SRPO asks the model to self-localize the first erroneous reasoning step and resets there, requiring no external process labels. The theory section formalizes this through Conservative Policy Iteration. CPI-RR samples random reset states, while CPI-CARO assumes an oracle that tests whether a state is improvable, meaning that it admits an action whose advantage exceeds a threshold τ. This oracle construction clarifies why targeted resets can be much more sample-efficient than random resets, and SRPO is an approximate practical version using self-localization.","experiments":"The empirical suite spans ten reasoning benchmarks covering math, science, strategic reasoning, and commonsense reasoning. SRPO outperforms GRPO and contemporary baselines based on self-correction or shared-prefix continuation. On LiveCodeBench, SRPO reaches a higher pass rate and learns 2–3 times faster than GRPO and RRPO. The most informative ablation concerns localization quality: clean prefixes correct nearly twice as often as erroneous prefixes. This supports the main mechanism. Resets help, but their benefit depends strongly on whether the selected state is actually a useful point for intervention.","reading_guide":"Read closely. Start with the motivation and Figure 1 for the credit-assignment framing. Then inspect the algorithmic details of RRPO and SRPO, especially how suffix groups are formed and why only suffix tokens receive updates. In the theory section, focus on CPI-RR, CPI-CARO, and Theorem 1 to understand where the pπ factors come from. In the experiments, prioritize the aggregate reasoning benchmark table, the LiveCodeBench learning curves, and the self-localization quality ablations. The main open questions are whether incorrect self-localization can create systematic negative updates, how suffix-only updates interact with KL control, and whether reset-based learning can be moved earlier into mid-training or reasoning data generation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应把 reset 当作 RLVR credit assignment 的最小可复现实验；先看算法和保守更新分析，再决定是否进内部 ablation。","en":"Treat resets as a minimal RLVR credit-assignment ablation; read the algorithm and conservative-update analysis before porting it internally."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Ankur Samanta","Akshayaa Magesh","Ayush Jain","Youliang Yu","Daniel Jiang","Kavosh Asadi","Kaveh Hassani","Paul Sajda","Jalaj Bhandari","Yonathan Efroni"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["rlvr","credit-assignment","reasoning","policy-optimization","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25507","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.25507","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.24517","title":"ECHO: Terminal Agents Learn World Models for Free","abstract":"CLI agents are the closest thing language models have to an embodied setting: the model emits commands, the terminal executes them, and the returned stream -- stdout, errors, files, logs, and traces -- records the consequences. We argue that this stream is a supervision signal, but standard agent RL discards it: GRPO-style training updates action tokens with sparse outcome-level rewards while ignoring environment responses already in the rollout. Failed rollouts provide little policy-gradient si","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-05-23T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_24517.png","hf_upvotes":6,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是终端 Agent RL 中训练信号过稀的问题。标准 GRPO 只在 assistant action token 上用最终成功/失败奖励做更新，而 rollout 里大量 terminal output、错误栈、日志、文件内容、测试结果只作为上下文参与前向计算，不进入 loss。失败轨迹虽然拿不到有用的 outcome reward，却包含环境如何响应当前命令的直接证据。ECHO 要把这部分环境反馈转成可训练的密集监督。","importance":"对预训练和 post-training 接口的启发在于，它把 Agent 交互日志重新看成一种在线语言建模数据，而不是只看成 RL rollout。贡献不在于新的 benchmark 或更复杂的 agent scaffold，而在于训练目标的重新分配：action token 用策略梯度，environment observation token 用交叉熵。这个思路接近“在交互中学习世界模型”，但实现很轻，不需要额外 teacher、额外 rollout 或独立 world model。对于基座研究员更有价值的一点是：它说明 RL 阶段可以持续引入自监督建模目标，缓解 sparse reward 下大量 token 被 mask 掉的问题。这类目标可能也适用于代码执行、浏览器、工具调用、多模态环境反馈等场景。","technical_approach":"ECHO 的训练序列由 system prompt、user task，以及多轮 assistant action 和 environment observation 交替组成。标准 GRPO 只在 assistant-action 位置集合 A 上计算策略梯度损失。ECHO 在此基础上增加 environment-token cross-entropy，对终端输出位置 O' 计算 next-token 预测损失。也就是说，同一个 transcript 里，模型对自己生成的命令 token 承担 RL loss，对环境返回的 stdout、stderr、exit code、文件片段、日志和 traceback 承担语言建模 loss。\n\n关键点有三个。第一，环境 token 不是模型采样出来的 action，但它们已经在完整 transcript 的 teacher-forced forward 中有 logits，因此可以直接取对应位置的 NLL，不需要第二次前向。第二，监督是 on-policy 的：环境输出来自当前策略实际执行的命令。随着策略访问新的状态，环境自动产生新的预测目标，形成随能力变化的课程。第三，这个辅助目标要求模型追踪隐含环境状态，例如文件是否被改过、测试为何失败、某个命令会暴露什么日志。它不是直接奖励成功动作，而是让模型更会预测“命令之后会发生什么”。这和只加更多 expert SFT 的区别在于，ECHO 不依赖人工或强模型示范；和只做 GRPO 的区别在于，失败轨迹也能提供 token-level 学习信号。","experiments":"实验在 TerminalBench-style Docker 任务环境中进行，起始模型包括 Qwen3-8B、OpenThinker-Agent-v1-SFT 和 Qwen3-14B。论文给出的一个训练背景是，在 Qwen3-8B 设置下，on-policy rollout 的成功率常低于 15%，这正是 GRPO 信号稀疏的来源。\n\n主要结果集中在 TerminalBench-2.0 和内部 held-out evaluation。公开 TerminalBench-2.0 上，Qwen3-8B 从 GRPO 的 pass@1 2.70% 提升到 ECHO 的 5.17%；Qwen3-14B 从 5.17% 提升到 几乎不改变这项指标。这个实验重要，因为它把性能提升和“学到终端动态”联系起来，而不是只报告 pass rate。另一个结果是，从 base Qwen3-8B 出发，ECHO 在内部评测上可以匹配 OpenThinker-SFT+GRPO，且没有使用 OpenThinker 背后的约 15k expert demonstrations；在 TerminalBench-2.0 上，它缩小了大约一半 expert-SFT gap。论文还报告了 verifier-free adaptation：即使没有 verifier，只靠与环境交互并预测环境响应，策略有时也能改善。","reading_guide":"建议精读 §3 的目标函数设计，尤其是 action-token GRPO loss 和 observation-token CE loss 如何在同一次前向中共享 logits，以及 环境中，环境预测目标本身可能成为持续学习信号，但也要注意它不能替代任务成功奖励；预测环境和完成任务之间仍有目标不一致的风险。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a specific inefficiency in terminal-agent RL: most of the interaction transcript is observed by the model but ignored by the loss. Standard GRPO updates assistant action tokens using sparse, delayed, often binary task outcomes. Terminal outputs such as stdout, stderr, logs, stack traces, file contents, and test failures are used only as context for the next action. Failed rollouts therefore provide little policy-gradient signal, even though they contain direct evidence about how the environment responded to the agent’s commands.","importance":"The main contribution is a training-objective change, not a new scaffold or benchmark. ECHO treats agent rollouts as both RL data and online language-modeling data. Action tokens receive the usual policy-gradient loss, while environment-observation tokens receive a cross-entropy prediction loss. This is relevant to pretraining and post-training research because it shows a clean way to reintroduce dense self-supervised learning into RL-based agent training. The method is lightweight: no teacher model, no extra rollout budget, and no separate world-model module. The broader implication is that tool-use, code execution, browser interaction, and multimodal agent environments may already contain dense supervision that current RL pipelines mask out.","technical_approach":"$22","experiments":"$23","reading_guide":"Read Section 3 carefully. ca","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T09:30:49+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25629.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是 preference category 组织，例如 helpfulness、harmlessness；每个 category 下包含多个不同来源的数据集，它们在 prompt 分布、response 风格、标注协议和数据伪特征上都不同。训练时只在某一个 source dataset 上构造 W2S：先用 gold labels 训练弱监督器 w_S，再让它给 student split 打软标签，强学生 m_S 只用这些弱标签训练；同时训练一个同容量的 strong ceiling m_S^gt 作为 gold-supervised 上界。评测时既看 source test，也看同一 category 下其他数据集的 zero-shot transfer。这样可以把“源域拟合”和“同类偏好迁移”拆开。\n\n第二部分是 Representation Anchoring。论文的核心判断是：弱监督 fine-tuning 会把强模型的内部表示拉向弱教师更依赖的源域特征，导致原本更通用的偏好表征被覆盖。Anchor 的做法不是改 Bradley–Terry reward objective，而是在训练 reward model 时额外约束学生表示不要偏离 pretrained strong model 太远。直观上，它保留了强模型预训练/先验对齐阶段已经形成的广义语义结构，只允许在此基础上做有限适配，而不是完全追随弱标签。这个设计和常见的 logit distillation、KL-to-reference、weight regularization 有相似精神，但重点放在 representation space，而不是输出分布或参数范数。论文从表征漂移角度解释 W2S 失败模式，这一点比单纯报一个正则项更有信息量。","experiments":"从全文可见，作者做了跨 domain、跨 dataset、跨 model family 的系统实验，重点结论很稳定：现有 W2S 方法在 in-distribution 上可以接近甚至匹配 strong gold ceiling，但一旦换到同类偏好下的其他数据集，性能会明显下滑。引言里给出的代表性例子是：在 transferable preference representations, or does it merely fit source-domain preference patterns that look good only in-distribution? Prior W2S evaluations are mostly done under matched train/test distributions, so a strong student can appear successful even if weak supervision has pulled it toward dataset-specific artifacts. The authors study this in reward modeling, where a strong student is trained only on weak preference labels and then evaluated under zero-shot preference shift across datasets within the same broad category.","importance":"Its value is less about reward models alone and more about a general training pathology relevant to pretraining and post-training interfaces. That matters for weak supervision, synthetic-label training, distillation, RLAIF, continual pretraining, and any setting where a strong base model is adapted through imperfect signals.
