In-domain consolidation：同领域内的正确 rollouts 互为正样本，拉近表示，促使领域内可复用解题结构被巩固。\n  - Cross-domain sharing：从其他领域挑选“可迁移的正确 rollouts”作为正样本，同时把错误 rollouts 作为负样本，推动跨领域共享的推理结构在表示空间中对齐。\n- 用一个轻量 contrastive head 把 rollout 映射到紧凑表示空间，采用 InfoNCE 风格损失；再与 prompt 内的 GRPO loss 组合训练。\n关键区别在于：它不只是在标量 reward 上做平衡，而是显式学习一个“正确推理轨迹的几何结构”，用来承载迁移与抗干扰。","experiments":"从片段可确认的结论级信息：MCPO 在多领域推理上实现更一致的提升，并且在部分设置下优于单领域训练。片段未给出具体基座模型、领域集合、训练步数/采样数、以及各 benchmark 的数值表。\n评估时建议重点核对三类结果：\n- 是否真的做到“各领域都不掉”（而非平均分上升）。\n- 对比对象是否包含强的多领域 GRPO 变体（重加权/梯度平衡等）。\n- 消融：只做 in-domain CL、只做 cross-domain CL、以及不同正样本挖掘策略对稳定性的影响。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（预计 30–45 分钟）：\n- 先看方法动机那部分：他们如何定义“可迁移的正确 rollout”，以及为什么把 rollouts 类比成数据增强。\n- 重点看 structured contrastive pool 的构造细节：正负样本怎么选、跨领域正样本如何判定“transferable”，以及 batch 内/跨 batch 的 memory bank 设计（如果有）。\n- 再看损失组合与训练稳定性：contrastive loss 权重、是否影响 GRPO 的 advantage 估计方差。\n- 实验部分优先看 per-domain 曲线与干扰案例，而不是只看平均分；如果提供了表示空间可视化或检索式分析，也值得快速扫一眼验证“对齐”是否发生。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In multi-domain RL post-training (math/code/safety, etc.), GRPO-style optimization with a single shared objective often causes cross-domain interference: gains in one domain come with regressions in others. Prior work mainly reduces conflicts via sampling control, reward normalization, reweighting, or gradient balancing, but lacks an explicit mechanism for knowledge sharing across domains. The paper addresses how to turn cross-domain interaction from competition into transferable learning.","importance":"This is a post-training contribution, yet it is relevant to foundation-model training as an interface idea: it augments scalar-reward RL with a structured representation-learning signal over rollouts. For multi-task continual training and alignment, the message is practical: when heterogeneous objectives fight each other, adding explicit geometric/contrastive constraints over “good trajectories” can be more stable than tuning mixture weights alone.","technical_approach":"MCPO adds a structured contrastive objective on top of GRPO:\n- Sample multiple rollouts per prompt under mixed-domain batches and score them with domain-specific rewards to identify correct/incorrect trajectories.\n- Build a structured contrastive pool from the same rollout batch.\n  - In-domain consolidation: treat correct rollouts within the same domain as positives to cluster reusable solution structures.\n  - Cross-domain sharing: select correct rollouts from other domains as positives (intended to be transferable), while using incorrect rollouts as negatives.\n- Map rollouts into a compact embedding space via a lightweight contrastive head and optimize an InfoNCE-style loss.\n- Combine the contrastive loss with the standard within-prompt GRPO objective.\nThe key difference from prior multi-domain RL tweaks is that MCPO explicitly learns a representation geometry for “good reasoning trajectories,” aiming to support transfer and reduce interference beyond scalar reward aggregation.","experiments":"From the provided excerpt, the claimed outcomes are: more consistent improvements across multiple reasoning domains, and in some settings outperforming single-domain training. We analyze this drift through dominant singular directions of parameter updates and show that reward-hacking runs exhibit substantially larger directional change tha","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-24T17:34:34+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25189.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 RL for LM 里的 reward hacking，但切入点不是继续改 reward model，而是问：为什么模型会沿着“看起来 reward 更高、真实能力却更差”的方向走偏。作者把问题表述成优化几何问题：干净训练往往沿着一个相对稳定、低维的更新轨迹前进，而 reward hacking 对应的是更新方向从这条轨迹上发生明显旋转和漂移。论文要解决的核心问题，就是能否用这种“方向一致性”来识别并约束 RL 更新，从而延缓 shortcut exploitation。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关联不如 post-train 强，但它对理解 RL 阶段与基座参数空间几何的关系有启发。很多关于 reward hacking 的工作把问题归结为 reward misspecification，这篇文章补了另一个维度：即使 reward 不完美，模型是否会偏离一条相对可信的学习子空间，可能本身就是风险信号。这个视角对 RLHF、RFT、reasoning RL 都有参考意义，尤其是在大家越来越关心“RL 到底学到了什么，而不是只看 reward 曲线”的背景下。贡献量级更像一个有意思的机制假设加一个简单有效的干预方法，而不是已经成熟的通用 recipe。","technical_approach":"方法分两步。第一步是分析更新方向。作者把训练过程中某一步的参数更新 ΔW 做 SVD，写成若干奇异方向的叠加，重点关注前 K 个 dominant directions。直觉上，这些方向解释了更新中最主要的变化模式。为了比较不同 checkpoint 的方向是否一致，作者不用简单向量夹角，而是对由 top-k 奇异向量张成的子空间做 CCA，相当于测两个低维更新子空间的相似度。论文观察到，在不发生 hacking 的训练里，这些主导方向随时间演化较平滑；而在 hacking run 中，方向相似度显著下降，说明更新发生了旋转。第二步是基于这个观察做约束。作者提出 trusted-direction gradient alignment / trusted-direction projection：先用少量“干净”的监督训练步骤得到一组参考参数变化，对这些变化做 SVD，提取一个 trusted subspace；随后在 RL 训练时，把梯度投影到这个子空间内，只允许更新沿着这些被认为更可信的方向发生。这里的关键取舍是，作者只约束 output-side 的主导方向，不强行固定全部参数更新，因此比强 KL 回拉 reference policy 更有机会保留学习能力。与已有方法相比，它不是改 reward，也不是单纯缩小步长或做平滑，而是显式控制更新方向。","experiments":"从给出的正文内容看，核心实验集中在数学推理场景下的人为 reward-hacking 设置。分析部分先比较 clean run 和 hacking run 的方向一致性，使用 rank-1 CCA similarity，例如比较 step 20 和 step 80 的主导更新方向。论文报告 clean run 在大多数模块上的 CCA 大约维持在 0.8 左右，而 hacking model 明显更低，且最差层的漂移更严重。这支持“reward hacking 伴随方向漂移”的经验观察。方法部分再把 trusted-direction projection 用到 RL 训练中，主结论是它能显著延缓 shortcut exploitation，并更好保持真实任务性能。摘要里用的是 delay reward hacking 和 preserve task performance 这样的表述，说明它更像缓解而不是彻底消除。就目前可见内容，论文没有展示特别大规模的模型或超长训练，而是更偏机制验证：先证明几何信号存在，再证明基于该信号的投影约束有用。阅读时要留意作者是否报告了不同 rank K、不同 trusted data 规模、以及与 KL regularization 或 sharpness-based 方法的对比，这些决定方法是否只是特定设置下有效。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction，把作者和 reward misspecification 路线、sharpness 路线的区别理清。然后直接看 Dominant Update Directions 和 Directional Shift in Reward Hacking，这两节给出整篇文章最重要的机制假设：reward hacking 不是只看 reward 设计，还要看更新是否偏离稳定子空间。接着看方法节里 trusted subspace 如何由少量 clean SFT steps 构造，以及 RL 时具体投影在哪些层、哪些矩阵上。最后看实验图，重点关注 CCA 曲线、真实任务性能与 proxy reward 是否出现背离、以及投影后这种背离是否被推迟。预计 25 分钟能完成泛读。如果要判断是否值得复现，优先检查附录里 rank 选择、不同模块一致性、以及投影带来的优化开销。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies reward hacking in RL for language models, but instead of treating it purely as a reward-modeling problem, it asks a geometric question: why do some RL runs drift into directions that improve proxy reward while hurting true task performance? The authors frame clean learning as following a relatively stable low-dimensional update trajectory, and reward hacking as a directional departure from that trajectory. The core problem is whether this notion of directional consistency can be measured and then used to constrain RL updates so that shortcut exploitation is delayed.","importance":"Its connection to pretraining is indirect, but the paper is relevant to anyone interested in how post-training interacts with the geometry of a pretrained model. Much of the reward-hacking literature focuses on misspecified rewards. This work adds another axis: even with imperfect rewards, the model may become unsafe when optimization leaves a trusted learning subspace. That is useful for RLHF, reasoning RL, and reinforcement fine-tuning more broadly, especially when reward curves are no longer a reliable proxy for actual capability. The contribution is best viewed as a mechanistic hypothesis plus a simple intervention, not yet a fully established general recipe.","technical_approach":"$25","experiments":"$26","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction to separate this paper from the standard reward-misspecification framing and from sharpness-based mitigation. After that, inspect how the trusted subspace is built from a small number of clean SFT steps and exactly where the projection is applied during RL. In the experiments, focus on the CCA plots, the divergence between proxy reward and true task performance, and whether the projection delays that divergence. A","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T08:26:34+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25571.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是多模态推理模型在后训练阶段很容易遇到的一个具体瓶颈：静态 SFT/RL 数据很快被模型吃干，后续提升缺少针对性；而纯自演化合成数据虽然能持续扩展难度，却容易把错误推理路径、幻觉知识和教师分布偏差一并放大。论文想解决的不是单次训练技巧，而是如何让后训练数据随着模型能力一起演化，同时又不把训练过程带偏。它把问题落在“失败前沿”上：先找出模型当前最容易出错、但又最有提升空间的区域，再用可验证的真实知识作为锚点去扩展训练样本，最后把外部提示逐步蒸馏成模型自身能力。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它虽然是多模态 training、mid-train curriculum、以及 RL 不能凭空创造新能力的讨论都有直接参考价值。贡献量级上，它更像一个扎实的训练框架与数据构造范式，而不是新的基础模型架构。","technical_approach":"方法由三个环节组成，而且三者是串起来的。第一步是 Failing-Frontier Discovery。作者不是直接在全量种子集上做扩充，而是先让当前模型对种子数据做多次 rollout，观察哪些题目稳定失败、哪些推理轨迹暴露出明显短板。随后再用教师模型对这些失败样本做诊断，抽取失败相关的关键词、知识点和提示信息。这样做的目的，是把后续数据构造对准模型当前能力边界，而不是泛泛增加训练量。\n\n第二步是 Truth Anchor Expansion。这是全文最关键的设计。作者不信任闭环自生成数据的事实性和逻辑一致性，因此引入外部真实数据库，从失败样本对应的知识点出发检索“truth anchors”。这些 anchor 不是直接当答案，而是作为高保真参考，用来构造新的训练样本、补充背景知识、校正推理链中的事实节点。和常见 self-evolution 不同，这里数据扩展的约束来自外部可验证知识，而不是教师模型自由发挥。这样可以减少 synthetic QA 中常见的逻辑错配、答案漂移和越迭代越偏的问题。\n\n第三步是 Scaffold-Stripping Mechanism。作者把教师提示显式写进训练样本，先形成 scaffold-augmented supervision，让模型在带脚手架的条件下学会正确推理路径；然后再通过 RL 去掉这些脚手架，让模型不依赖固定提示模板也能完成推理。这里的分工很清楚：SFT 负责把新知识和正确轨迹灌进去，RL 负责把外显提示内化成行为习惯。这个设计背后有一个明确判断：如果直接用 RL 从稀疏正确率奖励里学复杂多模态推理，信号太弱；如果只做 SFT，模型又容易记住模板而不是形成稳定能力。\n\n从方法定位看，AnE 不是单纯的数据检索增强，也不是普通 teacher-student 蒸馏。它更像一个面向 reasoning frontier 的迭代式训练循环：失败诊断决定学什么，truth anchor 决定学的内容是否可信，scaffold stripping 决定能力能否脱离外部支架独立存在。","experiments":"论文以 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 为底座，在 8 个多模态推理 benchmark 上评测。文中给出的总结果是：相对 base model，AnE 多轮迭代后平均提升 10.3%。作者特别强调在 MathVision 和 EMMA 这类较难数据集上保持了领先表现，说明收益不只来自简单题或分布内题目。\n\n从实验结构看，最值得注意的不是单个分数，而是“持续提升”这一点。作者把 AnE 和静态数据后训练、以及纯 self-evolution 路线做比较，结论是静态数据方法很快进入平台期，而 AnE 在多轮迭代中还能继续涨，说明 failing frontier 驱动的数据更新确实在不断对准新短板。另一组关键对比是 truth anchor 的作用：去掉真实锚点后，性能提升会变弱，而且更容易出现 reasoning drift，说明外部可验证知识不是锦上添花，而是闭环演化能否稳定的核心条件。\n\n脚手架机制的消融也很重要。只做 scaffold SFT 而不做后续 RL，模型会更依赖提示格式，泛化和自由生成能力不足；只做 RL 而没有前面的 scaffold supervision，则很难稳定学到正确轨迹。两者结合时效果最好，和作者在引言里对 SFT 与 RL 分工的判断一致。\n\n受限于给出的全文片段，部分 benchmark 的逐项数字、迭代轮数细节、以及各消融的精确幅度没有完全展开；但从正文描述可以确认，实验重点是验证三件事：一是相对静态数据的持续增益，二是相对纯合成演化的数据质量优势，三是 SFT 注入 + RL 内化这一训练分工的必要性。","reading_guide":"如果只花一小时，优先看三处。第一是方法总览图和 Failing-Frontier Discovery 部分，理解作者如何定义“失败前沿”，以及为什么要用多次 rollout 而不是单次错误样本筛选。这里关系到整个框架是不是在做真正的 targeted training。第二是 Truth Anchor Expansion 的实现细节，重点看 anchor 的来源、检索条件、如何从 anchor 构造成新样本，以及它和教师生成内容各自承担什么角色。这里决定这篇工作的含金量。第三是 scaffold-stripping 的训练流程和消融表，判断 RL 到底是在提升正确率，还是在做模板去除与行为内化。\n\n图表上建议重点看：总框架图、不同迭代轮次的性能曲线、truth anchor 与无 anchor 的对比、以及 scaffold/RL 相关消融。如果有案例分析，优先看失败样本如何被诊断、检索、再构造成训练数据，这能直接看出方法是否真的避免了 synthetic drift。\n\n值得深挖的点有三个。其一，truth anchor 的质量上限由外部数据库覆盖率决定，这会不会把方法限制在可检索知识密集的任务上。其二，教师模型在失败诊断和提示生成中仍然扮演重要角色，教师偏差是否会通过 scaffold 间接注入。其三，这套框架能否迁移到纯文本 reasoning continual pretrain，或者更一般的 mid-training data refresh 场景。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a concrete bottleneck in multimodal reasoning post-training: static SFT/RL datasets saturate quickly, while self-evolving synthetic data can keep expanding but often amplifies logical errors, hallucinated reasoning paths, and teacher-distribution mismatch. The real problem is not just data scarcity. It is how to keep training data adaptive to the model’s current failure frontier without letting the optimization loop drift away from reliable knowledge. AnE frames this as a closed-loop evolution problem: identify where the model currently fails, retrieve verified external anchors around those failures, and then convert teacher-guided reasoning into internalized capability.","importance":"For pretraining-oriented readers, the value is less about multimodal benchmark chasing and more about training-signal design. The paper is a useful case study in how data quality, adaptivity, and distribution alignment interact in iterative training. It also reinforces a view that has become increasingly important in reasoning work: SFT is better at injecting new patterns and knowledge, while RL is better at consolidating and de-scaffolding behavior. In that sense, AnE is relevant to continual pretraining, mid-training curriculum refresh, and any setting where synthetic data loops risk compounding model errors. The contribution is a practical training paradigm rather than a new base architecture.","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"$28","reading_guide":"$29","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"后训练里少见地把“失败前沿定位 + 真值锚定”讲成了可执行 recipe；先看数据构造部分，若 setup 干净，值得迁到 reasoning data pipeline 里试。","en":"One of the few post-training papers that turns 'failure-frontier mining + ground-truth anchoring' into an actionable recipe; read the data-construction part first and port it if the setup is clean."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zehao Wang","Yihan Zeng","Zidong Gong","Yuanfan Guo","Feng Zhu","Hongzhi Zhang","Wei Zhang","Wangmeng Zuo"],"notable_authors":["Wangmeng Zuo"],"tags":["multimodal-reasoning","post-training","synthetic-data","self-evolution","ground-truth-anchoring"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25571","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.25571","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.25582","title":"Extreme Region Policy Distillation","abstract":"Reinforcement learning for large language models faces a fundamental trade-off between sample efficiency and asymptotic performance: strictly on-policy methods discard trajectories after a single update, while off-policy reuse introduces distribution mismatch that existing trust-region techniques mitigate primarily by enforcing conservative optimization, often leaving rich training signals underutilized. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T08:32:24+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25582.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 LLM 强化学习中的样本效率和 KL 效率冲突。严格 on-policy 训练每批轨迹通常只用一次，采样成本高；off-policy 重复更新可以提高样本利用率，但会让当前策略偏离生成数据的旧策略，引入分布错配。PPO 类 KL 或 ratio clipping 能抑制漂移，但也会使优化过于保守。作者关心的问题是：能否充分挖掘一批轨迹里的训练信号，同时避免无效策略漂移消耗 KL 预算。","importance":"这篇论文更接近 post-train，但对预训练研究有两个启发。第一，它把 reasoning RL 的瓶颈从“有没有更多数据”推进到“每批轨迹中的有效信号如何被利用”。这对长 CoT 采样成本很高的模型尤其重要。第二，它强调 KL divergence 不只是正则项，而是一种训练预算。无效漂移会占用预算，却不带来任务收益。这个视角对 mid-train、RL pretrain、持续训练中的 trust region 设计都有参考意义。贡献量级主要是训练范式和经验观察，不是基础架构变化。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Extreme Region Policy Distillation。它把一次 RL 更新拆成两个阶段。第一阶段先放松 KL 约束，在固定轨迹数据上做更激进的多步 off-policy 优化，目标是尽量提取这批数据中的训练信号。这个阶段得到的策略不直接作为最终模型，而是作为 teacher。第二阶段把 teacher 中有用的监督信号蒸馏回原始策略，并显式加 trust-region 约束，让 student 保留有效改进，同时过滤掉第一阶段产生的多余漂移。作者还讨论了 strong teacher 和 weak teacher 两类情况。strong teacher 指第一阶段确实训练出更强策略；weak teacher 指即使 teacher 本身不更强，甚至是退化构造，也可能通过不同 signal construction strategy 给 student 提供有用方向。这个设计的关键不是简单复用 off-policy 数据，而是先在“极端区域”探索可用信号，再用受约束蒸馏把有效部分投回可接受的 KL 区域。","experiments":"实验主要在数学推理任务上进行，覆盖强基座模型在标准 on-policy RL 收益变小的场景。作者先做固定数据上的大量 off-policy 更新分析：早期多步优化能快速提升，但更新过多后，轨迹概率偏移增大，policy entropy 下降，性能很早进入平台期。单纯收紧 KL 约束不能解决问题，只会降低可达到的性能上限。两阶段蒸馏后，student 能在相近性能下使用更小 KL，说明一部分 off-policy 漂移不是有效任务改进。论文还报告了在 Qwen3.5-27B 级别强模型上的实验，显示 weak teacher strategy 在强模型继续提升困难时仍能带来收益。可见正文没有提供完整 benchmark 表和具体提升数字，因此更可靠的结论是训练动态和 KL-efficiency 观察。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 30 到 40 分钟。优先看固定轨迹多步 off-policy 更新的诊断图，尤其是 performance、trajectory probability、entropy、KL 的联动变化。然后看两阶段框架图，理解为什么 teacher 不是最终策略，而是中间信号提取器。方法部分重点看三类 signal construction strategy，以及 student 何时能超过 teacher。实验部分建议关注强模型上的结果，因为这类模型最能暴露 on-policy RL 的边际收益问题。若时间有限，可以跳过一般 RL 预备知识，直接读 off-policy degradation 分析和 distillation ablation。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies the tension between sample efficiency and KL efficiency in reinforcement learning for large language models. Strictly on-policy training usually uses each batch of trajectories once, which is expensive for long reasoning rollouts. Reusing trajectories off-policy improves sample utilization, but repeated updates move the policy away from the behavior policy that generated the data. Standard PPO-style clipping or KL penalties reduce this mismatch, but they also make optimization conservative. The central question is whether a training procedure can exploit more signal from each batch without wasting KL budget on ineffective policy drift.","importance":"This is primarily a post-training paper, but it is relevant to pretraining researchers who care about the interface between base models and reasoning RL. The useful idea is to treat KL divergence as a limited optimization budget, not just as a regularizer. Off-policy updates may consume this budget through probability drift and entropy collapse without producing durable task improvement. That observation matters for RL pretraining, mid-training, continual training, and any setting where rollout generation is costly. The contribution is a training procedure and a set of empirical diagnostics rather than a new model architecture.","technical_approach":"$2a","experiments":"$2b","reading_guide":"A skim reading should take about 30 to 40 minutes. Start with the diagnostic plots for repeated off-policy updates on fixed trajectories, especially the interaction among performance, trajectory probability, entropy, and KL. Then read the two-stage framework carefully: the teacher is an intermediate signal extractor, not the final policy. In the method section, focus on the signal construction strategies and the conditions under which the student can outperform the teacher. In the experiments, prioritize the results on strong models, because they best illustrate why standard on-policy RL leaves useful signal unused. The generic RL preliminaries can be skipped if the reader is already familiar with PPO, KL constraints, and off-policy mismatch.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 fixed-data off-policy 曲线；若现象稳，RLVR 采样复用的内部分层实验应复现。","en":"Read the fixed-data off-policy curves; if stable, this is worth reproducing in internal RLVR sample-reuse ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Changyu Chen","Xiting Wang","Rui Yan"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RLHF","RLVR","off-policy-learning","policy-distillation","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25582","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.25582","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.25198","title":"Hide to Guide: Learning via Semantic Masking","abstract":"Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful paradigm for improving language models on reasoning-intensive tasks, but its effectiveness is often limited by exploration. imp","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-24T17:59:06+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25198.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 RLVR 中专家轨迹指导和奖励泄漏之间的冲突。完整专家轨迹能帮助探索，但也可能暴露最终答案、中间数值、可执行代码或搜索实体，使策略通过复制关键内容拿到 verifier 奖励，而不是学习可泛化的推理或搜索过程。","importance":"它对预训练研究的价值主要在 post-train 与训练信号质量接口。RLVR 的有效性不仅取决于 verifier，也取决于模型在探索时看到了什么条件信息。论文把“专家轨迹不是纯正信号”讲得比较清楚：轨迹同时包含过程脚手架和可被奖励利用的内容。这个视角对构造 SFT/RL 数据、设计 reasoning trace、避免 reward hacking 都有启发，但方法本身是轻量的 guided RL 改造，不是新的预训练范式。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Semantic Masked Expert Policy Optimization，简称 SMEPO。它不改变 verifier、奖励函数或 GRPO-style RL 目标，而是在给策略的专家轨迹上做语义遮蔽。核心原则是保留过程结构，例如分解方式、子目标、推理流程和搜索路径；遮蔽与 verifier 目标强相关、位于关键路径上的内容。论文给出三个实例：数学中遮蔽数值内容，避免直接泄露最终答案和中间计算值；代码中遮蔽专家解法代码，避免直接复制可执行实现；agentic search 中遮蔽答案相关实体和 query pivots，减少通过实体匹配绕过搜索过程的机会。相比只给 partial prefix 或 adaptive hints，SMEPO 尝试保留更多专家过程信息，同时降低奖励泄漏。","experiments":"主实验采用每个领域 1k 样本设置，在 Qwen3-8B-Base 上，数学从 47.6 提升到 50.3，代码从 56.5 提升到 59.0，agentic search 从 45.6 提升到 48.8。迁移实验中，Qwen2.5-7B 数学从 42.4 提升到 是否在训练和推理之间形成新的分布差异。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a tension in RL with verifiable rewards: expert traces improve exploration, but full traces can leak verifier-aligned content. A policy may obtain reward by copying final answers, intermediate values, executable code, or answer-related entities, rather than learning the reasoning or search behavior that produced them.","importance":"The main relevance is to the interface between post-training and signal quality. RLVR is not only about the verifier and the RL objective; it also depends on what information the policy is conditioned on during exploration. The paper makes a useful distinction between procedural scaffolding and reward-relevant leakage inside the same expert trace. This is relevant for reasoning-trace design, SFT/RL data construction, and reward-hacking analysis, though the method itself is a lightweight guided-RL intervention rather than a new pretraining method.","technical_approach":"The proposed method is Semantic Masked Expert Policy Optimization, or SMEPO. It leaves the verifier, reward function, and GRPO-style objective unchanged, and instead modifies the expert trace provided to the policy. The rule is to preserve procedural structure such as decomposition, subgoals, reasoning flow, and search plans, while masking semantic spans that lie on the critical path to the verifier target. The instantiations are domain-specific: numerical content is masked in math traces, expert solution code is masked in coding traces, and answer-related entities are masked in agentic search traces. Compared with partial prefixes or adaptive hints, the method tries to keep more of the expert process while reducing direct reward leakage.","experiments":"In the main 1k-example-per-domain setting, SMEPO improves Qwen3-8B-Base from 47.6 to 50.3 on math, from 56.5 to 59.0 on code, and from 45.6 to 48.8 on agentic search. The gains also transfer to other math models: Qwen2.5-7B improves from 42.4 to 44.5, and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B improves from 61.8 to 62.6. The method reaches hard-math targets earlier than vanilla GRPO. In a GHPO setup using the original GHPO data, adding SMEPO raises the math average from 44.0 to 45.6. Ablations support the leakage explanation: random masking fails, fixed-prefix exposure is unstable, and more targeted removal of reward-relevant content works best.","reading_guide":"A focused skim should take about 25 to 35 minutes. Start with the Introduction, where the failure mode is defined clearly: sparse exploration rewards on one side and expert-trace leakage on the other. In the method section, focus on how masking targets are chosen for math, code, and agentic search; those design choices matter more than the RL notation. In the experiments, read the main result table, the hard-math convergence analysis, the GHPO combination result, and the masking ablations. For RLVR data work, the key questions are whether the masking rules depend too much on hand-built heuristics, whether they remove useful reasoning information, and whether masked traces create a train-test conditioning mismatch.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"针对 RLVR 中引入专家轨迹导致的 reward hacking 问题，提出了语义掩码的解决方案，对关注 RL 训练稳定性和数据合成质量的研究员很有启发。","en":"Addresses reward hacking caused by expert traces in RLVR using semantic masking; highly insightful for researchers focusing on RL training stability and data synthesis quality."},"metadata":{"institutions":["MIT"],"authors":["Ruitao Liu","Qinghao Hu","Alex Hu","Yecheng Wu","Shang Yang","Luke J. MoE-LoRA 里 gate 的表达能力过弱。传统 MoE 或 MoE-LoRA 的 router 只给每个专家一个标量权重，能做的是“放大或缩小某个专家的贡献”，但不能改变专家输出在表示空间中的方向。作者认为这会限制低秩专家的组合能力，尤其是在专家数有限、任务分布复杂的场景里，模型只能决定用多少专家，不能决定如何重组专家的低秩子空间。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接相关性不高，因为它主要是 PEFT 方向，不是预训练 recipe 或基础建模范式创新。但它有一个值得记住的点：低秩结构不仅能省参数，也给更丰富的 gating 形式留下了空间。作者利用 LoRA 的 rank-r 子空间，把原本在 d 维空间里代价很高的通用线性变换，压缩成在 r 维子空间里做可控旋转。这种思路对 MoE 设计有启发，尤其是当人们讨论 expert aggregation 是否只能是 scalar mixture 时，它提供了一个更细粒度但仍然便宜的替代方案。","technical_approach":"标准 MoE-LoRA 的形式是 W = W0 + Σ g_i B_i A_i，其中 A_i 把 d 维输入投到 r 维低秩空间，B_i 再映回 d 维。作者指出，如果只用标量 g_i，那么每个专家贡献的方向由 B_i A_i 固定，router 只能调节幅度。一个自然想法是给每个专家加一个 r×r 变换 G_i，写成 W = W0 + Σ B_i G_i A_i，这样就能在低秩子空间里做更丰富的变换；但若直接学习一般线性变换，参数和计算仍然偏大。\n\nRotMoLE 的关键设计是把 G_i 限制为 rotation-like 变换。对于 rank=2 的情形，直接用一个角度 θ_i 构造二维旋转矩阵；对于 r>2，作者再引入少量参数来指定一个临时旋转平面，然后在该平面内做旋转，得到对应的 r×r 旋转矩阵 R_i。最终形式是 W = W0 + Σ g_i B_i R_i A_i。这样每个专家除了标量 gate 之外，还有一个“方向调整”自由度，但额外参数量仍然很小：rank=2 时只需 dn 级别，r>2 时是 (d+r)n，而不是为每个专家显式学习完整的 r×r 或更高维变换。这个设计的核心不是数学上把旋转写出来，而是利用低秩瓶颈把复杂 gating 约束成一个足够便宜、又比 scalar gate 更有表达力的操作。","experiments":"从全文可见，实验重点是验证 RotMoLE 相比传统 MoE-LoRA 在复杂场景下是否更有效，尤其是多任务、多领域或多语言这类需要专家重组能力的设定。作者反复强调 candidate experts 有限时，旋转 gate 的收益更明显，因为它扩大了固定专家集合可张成的解空间。题面没有给出具体表格数字，但从论文结构和引言表述看，实验应包含：与 LoRA、MoE-LoRA 及不同 gating 变体的主结果对比；不同 rank、expert 数、top-k 设置下的消融；以及参数量与性能的权衡。若只看结论，RotMoLE 的卖点不是增加很多参数后换来提升，而是在很小额外开销下，让低秩专家组合更灵活。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读。先看 Introduction 里对 scalar gating 局限的论证，再看方法部分关于为什么把一般线性变换约束成 rotation。真正需要判断的是：这个约束是否合理，是否比直接增大 expert 数或 rank 更划算。阅读时重点看三处：1）参数复杂度推导，确认额外开销确实低；2）r=2 与 r>2 两种构造的差别；3）消融里 rotation gate 的独立贡献，而不是把收益混在更多参数或更复杂训练技巧里。预计 20–30 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。如果关心 MoE/LoRA 结构设计，可以再细看不同任务设置下收益是否稳定，以及当 expert 数足够大时旋转机制是否仍有边际价值。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific limitation in MoE-LoRA: the router usually assigns only scalar weights to selected experts. That means it can scale each expert’s contribution, but it cannot change the direction of the expert output in representation space. The authors argue that this is too restrictive when a small set of low-rank experts must cover diverse tasks or domains.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper; it is mainly a PEFT architecture tweak. Still, it contains one useful idea for model design: low-rank structure does not only reduce parameters, it also creates a cheap subspace where richer expert transformations become feasible. Instead of treating expert aggregation as scalar mixing only, the paper shows how to add a directional transformation inside the LoRA subspace at modest cost. That is a relevant design pattern for sparse adaptation and possibly for future MoE variants.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"The experiments are designed to show that RotMoLE improves over standard MoE-LoRA in settings where expert composition matters, such as multi-task, multilingual, or otherwise heterogeneous adaptation scenarios. The claimed advantage is strongest when the number of candidate experts is limited, because rotational gating enlarges the effective solution space spanned by a fixed expert set. The prompt does not include the full result tables, but the paper appears to compare against LoRA, MoE-LoRA, and alternative gating variants, with ablations over rank, number of experts, and top-k routing. The main empirical claim is efficiency of expressiveness: better expert recombination with only a small parameter overhead.","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Start with the motivation for why scalar gating is insufficient, then move to the method section where the authors justify using rotations instead of general linear transforms. The key question is whether the added structure is a good trade-off compared with simply increasing rank or adding more experts. Focus on three parts: the parameter/computation analysis, the difference between the rank-2 and rank>2 constructions, and the ablations isolating the effect of the rotation gate from other changes. A useful skim takes about 20–30 minutes. However, these dense representation spaces suffer from feature superposition, where multiple concepts are encoded in overlapping dimensions, ma","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T07:27:41+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25525.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决 从分析工具推进到训练约束工具。它不属于基座预训练流程本身，但和 continual pretrain、领域持续适配、mid-train 后的能力保持有关。关键贡献不是提出新的 SAE，而是把稀疏、过完备的 feature space 用作蒸馏锚点，使正则化更接近“保护特征”而不是“冻结参数”。这个方向值得关注，因为长期更新模型时，如何在不保存大量旧数据、不从头训练的条件下减少干扰，是基座维护中的实际问题。","technical_approach":"SAE-FD 分三步。第一，针对基座模型激活训练一个 Gated SAE，把原始 d 维稠密激活映射到 D 维稀疏特征空间，其中 D 远大于 d；Gated SAE 用 gate pathway 决定哪些 feature 激活，用 magnitude pathway 估计强度，并利用 pre-ReLU feature 保持梯度流。第二，每完成一个任务的 LoRA 微调后，在少量 anchor samples 上记录该模型的 per-token SAE feature activations，作为旧任务表征快照。第三，在后续任务训练时加入 sparse feature distillation loss：一部分保持稀疏特征方向的 cosine similarity，另一部分匹配 active features 的幅值。这样做比直接蒸馏隐藏状态更有针对性，因为只对被 SAE 分解出的活跃概念施加强约束。作者还引入 adaptive lambda，动态调节任务损失和蒸馏损失的相对贡献。训练新任务初期遗忘风险高，lambda 较强；模型逐渐收敛后放松约束，减少对新任务学习的阻碍。","experiments":"实验使用两个连续学习 benchmark，覆盖三个模型家族，并以 LoRA 顺序微调为主要设置。提供内容中给出的核心结果是 TRACE 上平均准确率 52.70%，Backward Transfer 为 -0.46%。相较最强已发表 baseline，平均准确率提高 2.3 个百分点，遗忘量减少 34%。这些数字说明 SAE feature space 的约束比稠密空间正则化更好地平衡了 stability 和 plasticity。完整实验表、SAE 规模、anchor 样本数、lambda 目标比例、不同层 SAE 选择等细节在提供内容中没有完全展开，精读时需要核对这些设置是否对结果敏感。","reading_guide":"建议泛读到半精读，约 45 hidden-state distillation、output distillation、固定 lambda、无 SAE feature sparsity 等消融。如果 SAE 训练成本和 anchor memory 没有被充分报告，需要谨慎评估它在大规模 continual pretrain 中的可用性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets catastrophic forgetting in continual learning for LLMs. Existing regularization methods constrain updates in weight space, gradient space, or output space, but these dense spaces contain superposed features. As a result, protecting old knowledge often also suppresses useful plasticity for new tasks.","importance":"The main relevance to pretraining research is the use of sparse autoencoder features as a training-time regularization space. This is not a base pretraining recipe, but it is relevant to continual pretraining, domain adaptation, and model maintenance after mid-training or task-specific tuning. The contribution is to turn SAE features from an interpretability artifact into a distillation anchor. The approach suggests that preserving sparse, disentangled feature activations may be a cleaner constraint than freezing parameters or matching dense hidden states.","technical_approach":"SAE-FD has three stages. First, a Gated SAE is trained on activations from the base model using diverse text, mapping a dense d-dimensional activation into an overcomplete sparse D-dimensional feature vector with D much larger than d. The gated design separates feature selection from magnitude estimation and keeps useful gradient flow through pre-ReLU activations. Second, after each task is learned with LoRA, the method records per-token SAE feature activations on a small set of anchor samples, producing a compact representation snapshot for previous tasks. Third, during later tasks, training combines the task loss with a sparse feature distillation loss. The distillation objective preserves feature direction through cosine matching and preserves active-feature strengths through magnitude matching. A dynamic lambda controller adjusts the weight of the distillation term to maintain a target contribution ratio, applying stronger protection early in a new task and relaxing the constraint as training stabilizes.","experiments":"The experiments cover two continual learning benchmarks and three model families under sequential LoRA adaptation. The reported TRACE result is 52.70% average accuracy with -0.46% backward transfer. Compared with the strongest published baseline cited by the authors, this is a +2.3 point gain in average accuracy with 34% less forgetting. The result supports the claim that sparse feature-space anchoring gives a better stability-plasticity tradeoff than dense-space regularization. The supplied content does not include all experimental details, so settings such as SAE size, anchor sample budget, target lambda ratio, layer choice, and SAE training cost should be checked before drawing deployment-level conclusions.","reading_guide":"Skim to semi-carefully read in about 45 minutes. Focus first on the method: how the Gated SAE is trained, how feature snapshots are stored, and how the distillation loss handles both direction and active-feature magnitude. The adaptive lambda mechanism is worth reading closely because a fixed regularization weight is often brittle in continual learning. In the experiments, prioritize the TRACE summary table, AA and BWT trajectories, and ablations against dense hidden-state distillation, output distillation, fixed lambda, and variants without sparse feature constraints. Existing works address this by credit allocation, including token-level advantage reweighting, and selective token optimization, however, the allocation cr","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T03:10:51+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25381.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，现有的信用分配（credit allocation）方法通常在整个训练过程中使用固定的分配标准，忽略了训练动态中不同阶段对不同策略行为的优化需求。","importance":"提出在 RLVR 优化中引入时间维度（temporal scheduling），指出“何时”调度学习信号与“何处”分配信号同样重要，为 RLVR 训练动态的控制提供了一个新的、正交的优化维度。","technical_approach":"提出在训练过程中动态调度信用分配标准：在训练初期优先优化由分配标准强调的特定 token（例如通过优势重加权或稀疏 token 优化），然后随着训练的进行逐渐衰减，过渡到一般的全 token 优化。此外，发现简单的轨迹百分比（trajectory percentiles）可以作为一种有效的调度标准，即先从后续 token（可靠的延续）开始调度，逐步纳入早期 token（可靠的推理脚手架）。","experiments":"在不同模型规模和 RLVR 算法（如 GRPO）上，结合连续优势重加权和离散稀疏 token 优化等现有信用分配策略，实验表明时间调度能带来一致的性能提升，并能有效缓解标准 RLVR 优化中因适应异构策略行为而导致的策略熵下降问题。","reading_guide":"建议重点关注 Introduction 中对现有静态信用分配局限性的分析，以及如何将时间调度与现有方法结合的具体实现细节。分析部分关于策略熵变化的讨论也值得一读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), existing credit allocation methods typically employ stagnant allocation criteria throughout the training process, neglecting the evolving optimization needs for different policy behaviors during training dynamics.","importance":"Introduces the temporal dimension to RLVR optimization, arguing that 'when' learning signals are scheduled is as crucial as 'where' they are allocated, providing a novel and orthogonal dimension for controlling RLVR training dynamics.","technical_approach":"Proposes to dynamically schedule credit allocation criteria over the course of training: prioritizing specific tokens emphasized by the allocation criteria (e.g., via advantage reweighting or sparse token optimization) in the early stages, and gradually attenuating towards general full-token optimization. designe","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-05-22T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_24218.png","hf_upvotes":35,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是单点能力，而是“通用 deep research agent 怎么训练”这个配方问题。作者把任务拆成三类彼此相关但训练信号不同的能力：fact seeking、citation grounding、report synthesis。难点在于，这三类能力横跨客观可验证任务和开放式主观任务，现有开源工作通常只覆盖其中一类，或者只有 SFT，没有把中期训练、长轨迹上下文管理、rubric 驱动的 RL 放进同一套流程里。Quest 试图回答的是：在完全开源、且大量依赖合成任务的条件下，能否训练出一个在长时程检索、证据归因和报告写作上都比较均衡的研究代理。","importance":"对预训练/后训练接口的启发大于对纯 pretrain 本身的直接推进。它的重要性主要有三点。第一，它把 deep research agent 的训练拆成可复现的 recipe：mid-training → SFT query-answer，而是“复杂查询 + task-specific rubric tree”。这个 rubric tree 用来刻画完成任务需要覆盖的子问题、证据要求和输出结构，因此它既是监督信号，也是后续 RL 的奖励依据。相比只合成最终答案，这种做法更适合长时程任务，因为 agent 的中间行为和最终报告都能被分层评估。\n\n第二是上下文管理。长轨迹 web research 的典型问题是上下文爆炸：搜索结果、网页片段、已发现事实、未解决子问题会迅速堆积。Quest 引入 compact summary 式的 context management，在轨迹推进过程中持续维护压缩后的信息状态，而不是把完整历史无差别塞回上下文。这个设计的意义不只是省 token，更是在训练时显式鼓励“记忆抽象”而不是“原文堆叠”。对 agent 来说，这比普通 RAG 式拼接更接近真实工作流。\n\n第三是训练流程。论文强调完整配方是 MT、SFT、RL 三阶段，而不是任选其一。mid-training 用来让底座模型适应 deep research 相关分布和行为模式；SFT 负责把任务格式、工具调用、报告输出对齐到目标接口；RL 则利用 rubric-based signal 做最后的能力整合，尤其补 report synthesis 和 citation grounding 这类难以靠 exact match 学好的目标。与已有开源系统相比，Quest 的差异不在某个单独模块，而在于把三类能力放进统一训练目标，并且让客观任务和开放任务共享一套 rubric 驱动优化框架。","experiments":"从文中给出的主结果看，Quest 覆盖 2B 到 35B 多个尺度，并在 8 个 benchmark 上评估三类能力。最关键的几个数字是：Quest-35B 在 BrowseComp 上 64.6%，在 Mind2Web 2 上 30.7%，在 DeepResearch Bench 上 48.2%，作者声称超过 OpenAI DeepResearch 等强闭源系统。这个结果如果按论文设定成立，说明统一 recipe 没有明显牺牲某一类能力来换另一类能力。\n\n更有信息量的是训练阶段消融。图 2 和正文都在强调，已有开源方法往往只做 fact seeking 或 report synthesis，且常缺 mid-training 或 rubric RL。Quest 的对比意图很明确：只做 SFT 的系统能力覆盖不完整；加入 MT 后，模型对搜索式任务和长轨迹状态管理更稳；再加 RL，开放式报告和引用约束进一步提升。虽然当前给出的全文片段没有完整消融表数字，但作者明确把 controlled studies 作为主要贡献之一，并且指出小模型也能受益：Quest-2B-SFT、Quest-4B-SFT 已经有“impressive performance”。这说明收益不只来自大模型容量，也来自训练信号设计。\n\n另一个值得注意的实验结论是 scaling。作者覆盖 2B、4B、9B 到 35B，意图展示 deep research agent 能力随模型规模提升，但不是只有大模型才可用。对隐私敏感场景，本地可部署的小模型 research agent 被单独点出，这意味着他们观察到 recipe 在小尺度上也有较好的 sample efficiency 或任务迁移性。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不必逐段细抠。建议先看 Introduction 里对三类能力的定义，以及 Figure 2 的训练配方对比，先建立作者到底在补什么缺口。然后重点看数据合成部分，尤其 Quest-8K 和 rubric tree 的构造方式；这里决定了后面 RL 信号是否可信。再看 context management 章节，关注它维护了什么状态、压缩频率如何、是否会丢失证据链。最后看训练与消融，重点确认三件事：MT 到底喂了什么数据；RL 的 reward 如何从 rubric tree 落到可执行打分；不同能力上哪一阶段贡献最大。\n\n如果时间只有 20–30 分钟，建议顺序是：Figure 1/2 → 数据合成章节 → 训练 recipe 章节 → 主结果表。若要深挖，优先看 rubric-based RL 的实现细节，因为这部分最接近可迁移的方法论；其次看小模型结果，判断这套配方到底是在“放大底座能力”，还是主要依赖大模型先验。","authors_note":"OSU activation patching, causal tracing, path patching, and steering in Transformers. It asks whether local interventions in the residual stream can be organized and predicted through sensitivity fields and propagation operators over depth and token position.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is mainly in mechanistic interpretability. Pretrained Transformer behavior is distributed across layers, tokens, heads, and residual subspaces; exhaustive patch enumeration is expensive and often hard to generalize. This work represents the residual stream as a depth-token field and formulates patching as localized source insertion. Linear response, empirical Green functions, and adjoint inverse problems provide a more structured way to describe and predict interventions. It is not a training method, but it may help build better diagnostic tools for pretrained models.","technical_approach":"The residual stream is treated as a field R(t,x), where t indexes depth and x indexes token position. A localized activation intervention is written as a source J(t,x). In a local linear regime, the first-order effect on an observable, such as a logit difference, is predicted by a sensitivity field a(t,x). The downstream propagation of a perturbation is described by an empirical Green-response operator G, which maps an injected perturbation at one depth-token site to residual or behavioral changes downstream. Patch selection is then formulated as a constrained inverse problem: given a desired residual displacement or behavioral shift, infer a sparse admissible source J whose propagated response matches the target. The adjoint variational view provides a route to infer patch sites and residual directions. This differs from standard patching by estimating reusable response quantities instead of measuring each intervention independently.","experiments":"The empirical tests use GPT-2-style autoregressive Transformers. The paper applies localized residual-field interventions and measures induced residual-field differences and logit-difference changes. The reported findings support a bounded local linear regime for small perturbations, approximate superposition, first-order sensitivity predictions of patch effects, structured anisotropic residual propagation, approximate composition across depth, and tractable lower-dimensional descriptions through high-sensitivity sites and sliced Green operators. Prompt-induced residual displacements partially transfer answer behavior. These are best read as first-pass validation of the response framework rather than evidence for large-scale model behavior. The main limitations are the locality of the linear approximation, nonlinear failure modes, and the still-speculative nature of cross-model response transfer.","reading_guide":"A practical skim takes about 20–30 minutes. Start with the definitions of the residual stream as a depth-token field and the mapping from patching to localized source insertion. Then focus on the roles of the sensitivity field and empirical Green-response operator. The most useful technical part is the constrained inverse formulation for patch-site inference; the continuum notation is less important than the discrete response quantities measured in finite Transformers. In the experiments, prioritize local linearity, superposition, sensitivity prediction, and sliced Green operator results. For pretraining researchers, the main question is whether these response measurements can become reusable diagnostics for large pretrained models; if the goal is training recipe improvement, this can be read at lower priority.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"用场论视角统一 Transformer 的 activation patching 和信息流分析，理论框架很优美，对深入理解模型内部机制和可解释性研究有启发，建议阅读方法部分。","en":"Unifies activation patching and information flow analysis in Transformers using a field-theoretic perspective; the theoretical framework is elegant and inspiring for deep understanding of internal mechanisms and interpretability research. These models may recognize individual attributes such as color and shape, but incorrectly combine attributes across objects, for example inferring a blue circle from a blue square and a red circle. The main goal is to explain why text-based pointing, where the model emits spatial coordinates before answering, helps with such failures.","importance":"The useful point for pretraining research is that the paper connects VLM failures to representational interference and attention mechanisms, rather than treating them only as data or instruction-following issues. This matters for multimodal training because intermediate supervision can shape internal algorithms, especially for grounding, object-wise reasoning, counting, and compositional visual understanding.","technical_approach":"The paper first analyzes a colored-shape visual search task, where the model must decide whether a target such as a green T is present among distractors that share partial target features. The authors link errors to attentional interference between objects with shared features. They then analyze Molmo-7B under pointing-via-text. Although internal attention states are not explicitly supervised, the model develops a serial search-like process while producing coordinate traces. The paper identifies a set of attention heads, called search heads, that implement this routine. Similar heads are reported in Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct, suggesting that the mechanism is not unique to one model family. The same type of mechanism is observed in Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct. On downstream-style controlled tasks such as counting and visual search, training models to point before answering reduces binding errors and improves out-of-distribution generalization. evidence","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T08:54:55+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25603.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是 CoT faithfulness 的实例级检测问题：一个模型生成的推理链看起来合理，甚至答案正确，但它是否真的反映了模型内部形成答案的计算过程。已有检测器主要看外部文本信号，例如推理是否自洽、答案是否一致、LLM judge 是否认为合理；这会把“推理质量”和“推理忠实性”混在一起。作者希望引入模型内部计算证据，但完整 circuit tracing 对长 CoT 成本太高，因此问题落在两个约束之间：既要利用内部机制信号，又不能为每条长推理链构建完整电路。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在训练 recipe，而在 CoT 作为训练与评测信号的可靠性。当前推理模型常把 CoT 当作 SFT、RL、过程监督、验证器训练的数据来源。如果 CoT 文本与内部决策过程系统性错位，那么用它训练过程奖励模型或做机制分析会引入噪声。CIE-Scorer 给出一个可操作的方向：不要只判断 CoT 文本是否正确，而是比较外显推理轨迹和内部计算轨迹是否对齐。这个思路对研究 reasoning trace 的可用性、RL 后训练信号质量、以及机制解释工具如何进入模型评测都有启发。","technical_approach":"方法叫 CIE-Scorer，核心是 internal-external discrepancy。它先对每个推理句子选择信息量较高的 token，而不是对整段 CoT 做完整 circuit tracing。这样可以降低长 CoT 上 attribution graph 的构建成本。随后，作者为每个句子构建局部计算图，把这些 sentence-level circuit graphs 压缩成内部推理 衡量两张图在结构和节点特征上的差异，并把这个距离作为 unfaithfulness score。与只看文本 plausibility 或 answer consistency 的方法相比，它显式使用模型内部计算证据；与完整 circuit tracing 相比，它用 token selection 和句子级图压缩换取可扩展性。","experiments":"实验在 FaithCoT-Bench 压缩是否优于直接使用浅层 hidden states，以及 FGW 距离是否比普通 embedding distance 更适合比较内部/外部轨迹。可见正文没有给出可核验的绝对 F1/AUC 数字，但实验结论强调，内部计算轨迹与外部文本轨迹的差异提供了外部文本判别器难以捕捉的信号。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20 到 30 分钟。重点看方法部分的三处设计：第一，如何选择 failure cases。如果关心 RL reasoning 数据质量，可以把它当作 CoT 可信度过滤器的候选机制，而不是当作完整 interpretability 方案来读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies instance-level detection of unfaithful chain-of-thought reasoning. A generated rationale may be coherent, plausible, and even paired with a correct answer, while still failing to reflect the computation that actually produced the model’s decision. Prior detectors mostly rely on external textual cues such as rationale quality, answer consistency, or LLM-as-judge scores. The missing signal is the model’s internal computation. Full circuit tracing could provide such evidence, but it is too expensive for long CoT traces. The paper targets this gap: use internal mechanistic evidence without constructing full circuits for every reasoning trace.","importance":"The relevance to pretraining and reasoning-model research is indirect but meaningful. CoT traces are often used as supervision for SFT, process reward models, verifiers, and RL training. If these traces are unfaithful, they can become noisy or misleading training signals. CIE-Scorer offers a concrete way to separate reasoning quality from faithfulness by comparing the displayed reasoning trajectory with an internal computational trajectory. This is useful for studying the quality of reasoning traces, filtering post-training data, and connecting mechanistic interpretability tools with evaluation.","technical_approach":"$31","experiments":"The experiments are conducted on four datasets from FaithCoT-Bench, with FINE-CoT serving as the main benchmark for instance-level unfaithfulness detection. The key ablations examine whether token-selected circuit tracing preserves useful internal evidence, whether compressed sentence-level circuit graphs outperform simpler hidden-state features, and whether Fused Gromov-Wasserstein distance is a better discrepancy measure than simpler embedding distances. The visible manuscript content does not expose exact F1 or AUC values, so the main verifiable takeaway is the relative improvement and the usefulness of internal-external mismatch as a signal.","reading_guide":"A skim reading is enough, around 20 to 30 minutes. 在深度方向发生 token oversmoothing 或 rank collapse 时，所有 token 最后会靠近什么方向。已有分析多讨论 self-attention 让 token 走向共识，但较少解释共识向量由哪些权重决定。作者把层索引看成离散时间，把注意力层近似为“矩阵乘法加归一化”，从而和幂迭代建立对应关系。","importance":"对预训练的价值主要在机制分析，而不是训练 recipe。它提供了一个可操作的谱分析视角：attention block 中 value/output 权重的乘积会放大某些主方向，层归一化则类似幂迭代里的归一化步骤。这个视角有助于理解深层模型中的 token 表征坍缩、共享权重模型的深度动力学，以及基于权重扰动的表示 steering。限制也很明确：分析主要忽略 FFN，并把 LayerNorm 简化为 L2 归一化，因此不能直接等价于完整 LLM 的前向传播。","technical_approach":"作者将 Transformer 视为沿层展开的离散动力系统。若暂时忽略 FFN，每层包含 value/output 投影、attention mixing 和归一化。幂迭代的形式是反复用矩阵 F 乘以向量并归一化；在 Transformer 中，对单头或多头 attention，可把关键的“层矩阵”写成 output weight 与 value weight 拼接矩阵的乘积。对于共享权重模型，如 ALBERT，F 在各层固定，系统接近 time-invariant dynamical system；当 token 已经接近共识时，full attention 矩阵接近均匀矩阵，attention 造成的扰动减弱，主特征向量方向会逐层被放大。对于 GPT-2、GPT-Neo、BERT 这类每层权重不同的模型，F 随层变化，不能期待收敛到单个固定主特征向量；作者改用统计方式观察 token 经过某层前后与该层主特征向量的相关性，检验是否存在平均意义上的放大。最后，作者提出一种 steering 方法：在最后一层的层矩阵上加入 rank-1 扰动，人工制造较大的谱间隔，让目标方向成为新的主特征向量，从而增强输出 token 表征与该方向的对齐。","experiments":"实验覆盖 ALBERT、GPT-2、GPT-Neo、BERT 等现成模型。对 ALBERT 这类共享权重模型，在使用 L2 归一化且去掉 FFN 的设置下，token 表征确实更清楚地向由 value/output 权重乘积决定的主特征向量靠近，这和幂迭代类比一致。对 GPT-2、GPT-Neo、BERT，作者观察到许多层都会增加 token 与当前层主特征向量的相关性，但对齐强度通常较弱，原因是这些层矩阵的第一、第二特征值之间谱间隔很小，主方向不会被强烈放大。rank-1 扰动实验说明，只要人为扩大谱间隔，token 可以被明显推向指定方向。提供内容未给出完整数值表，因此这部分更适合看作机制证据，而不是性能型结论。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 30–45 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对 oversmoothing 与幂迭代的连接，方法部分关于共享权重、full attention、symmetric value matrix 的解析结果，以及真实模型上“经过一层前后与主特征向量相关性变化”的图表。若关心可控生成或权重编辑，可看 rank-1 perturbation steering 部分。预训练研究者需要保留一个判断：这个分析对 attention block 的谱偏置有解释力，但忽略 FFN 后会低估 MLP 对表征重塑的作用，也没有直接回答训练过程中这些谱结构如何形成。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies what direction token representations approach when a deep Transformer exhibits oversmoothing or rank collapse. Prior work explains why self-attention can drive tokens toward consensus, but it says less about which vector defines that consensus. The authors model depth as discrete time and relate an attention layer, after dropping the FFN and using L2 normalization, to one step of the power method.","importance":"The value for pretraining research is mainly mechanistic. It gives a spectral lens on attention blocks: the product of value and output weights can amplify dominant representation directions, while normalization plays a role analogous to the normalization step in power iteration. This can inform analyses of depth dynamics, shared-weight Transformers, representation collapse, and weight-level steering. The scope is limited because the FFN is excluded and LayerNorm is approximated by L2 normalization, so the result should not be read as a full description of an operational LLM layer.","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"Experiments are run on off-the-shelf models including ALBERT, GPT-2, GPT-Neo, and BERT. For ALBERT, where weights are shared across layers, the predicted alignment with the dominant eigenvector of the value/output product is empirically visible under the simplified setting with L2 normalization and no FFN. For GPT-2, GPT-Neo, and BERT, many layers show an increase in alignment with the current layer’s dominant eigenvector, but the effect is usually weak because the leading spectral gaps are small. The rank-1 perturbation experiments show that once a large spectral gap is induced, token states can be strongly steered toward a target direction. To enable agent self-evolution, these trajectories can be distilled into reusable procedural skills that compactly encode experience to guide future behavior. We propose CODESKILL, an LLM-","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T05:12:49+00:00","category_id":"coding_agent","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25430.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"CODESKILL 解决的是编码 Agent 如何从历史交互轨迹中学习可复用技能，并把这些技能维护成一个能持续服务后续任务的技能库。关键点不是再训练下游编码 Agent，而是学习一个“技能管理策略”：决定从哪些轨迹证据中抽取什么、抽象到什么粒度、何时合并或删除已有技能。","importance":"这篇论文对基座预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train 与 agent self-improvement 有参考价值。它把经验蒸馏、记忆维护、技能更新从固定 prompt 和启发式规则，改成可训练策略，并用下游任务反馈优化。这对构造长期可积累的 Agent 训练信号有启发：轨迹不是只用于 SFT，也可以被压缩成过程性知识，再反过来改善冻结策略的执行。","technical_approach":"方法由三个部分组成。第一，维护一个技能库，每个技能是 markdown 形式的过程性指令，包含标题、触发条件和可执行建议。第二，给定历史轨迹和相关技能上下文，管理模型 Mθ 输出技能操作，包括生成新技能、基于新经验演化旧技能，以及维护技能库，例如添加、合并、删除。第三，训练这个管理模型。训练先用编码 Agent 轨迹和教师模型生成的技能操作做监督 warm start，再用 GRPO 优化。奖励由两类信号组成：一类是可验证的任务结果，检查技能是否帮助冻结的下游编码 Agent 解题；另一类是 rubric-based judge，评估技能质量以及技能与实际行为是否对齐。这个设计的取舍比较清楚：纯任务成功奖励太稀疏，纯 LLM judge 又容易偏离真实执行效果，两者结合能让管理策略同时学到可执行性和可复用性。","experiments":"论文用 Qwen3.5-4B 实例化 CODESKILL，在 EnvBench、SWE-Bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench 2 上评估。相对无技能基线，平均 pass rate 提升 9.69 个点；相对最强的 prompt-based 技能抽取或记忆构造基线，提升 4.01 个点。对应相对增益约为 33% 和 11%。结果覆盖域内和分布外软件工程任务，说明收益不只是来自记住训练任务细节。最有信息量的实验是冻结下游编码策略后，只改变技能库和技能管理方式，因为这能隔离“管理策略是否真的学会压缩和维护经验”这一问题。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对固定 prompt / heuristic memory 的问题定义，再看 CODESKILL 方法部分，重点是技能表示、操作空间、Update 机制和 GRPO 奖励设计。实验部分重点看三组对比：无技能、prompt-based 技能抽取、CODESKILL；如果有消融，优先看去掉任务奖励、去掉 rubric judge、去掉技能演化或维护操作后的变化。需要带着一个限制去读：该方法优化的是冻结下游 Agent 的外部技能库，不等价于模型权重层面的持续学习；技能是否能跨模型、跨工具栈迁移，需要额外验证。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"CODESKILL studies how a coding agent can turn past software-engineering trajectories into reusable procedural skills, and how those skills should be updated over time. The main problem is skill-bank management: what evidence to extract, how abstract the skill should be, and when to add, revise, merge, or drop skills, without updating the downstream coding agent itself.","importance":"The paper is not a direct pretraining contribution, but it is useful for post-training and agent self-improvement research. It reframes memory construction as a learnable policy rather than a set of fixed prompts and hand-written update rules. Each skill contains a title, a trigger condition, and actionable instructions. Given a trajectory and relevant skill-bank context, the management model Mθ emits an operation: generate a new skill, evolve an existing one with new or failed experience, or maintain the bank through adding, merging, or removing entries. Training has two stages. The model is first warm-started with supervised data built from coding-agent trajectories and teacher-generated skill operations. It is then optimized with GRPO. The reward mixes sparse verifiable task feedback from a frozen downstream coding agent with dense rubric-based judgments of skill quality and behavior-skill alignment. This is a reasonable compromise: task success is the right objective but sparse, while judge feedback is denser but needs grounding in execution outcomes.","experiments":"The authors instantiate CODESKILL with Qwen3.5-4B and evaluate it on EnvBench, SWE-Bench Verified, and Terminal-Bench 2. recovery hints it exposes) often stays static. This creates weak, non-diagnostic supervision: failures are detected but not explained, making learning inefficient and misaligned with the agent’s shifting capability boundary. The relevant takeaway for foundation-model researchers is about training-signal quality: converting sparse execution failure into turn-level diagnostic labels can be more impactful than simply scaling interaction data.
